Abstract: Multilingual language models develop shared cross-lingual representations, and various interpretability methods claim to quantify this sharing. These methods have been developed largely in isolation, and when they disagree, it is unclear whether the disagreement reflects a property of the model or an artifact of the measurement. We compare four sharing metrics (CKA, ANC, GMM dominance per token, and ILO) across 21 base models from five families (125M-14B parameters) and correlate each with cross-lingual transfer on five downstream tasks. We find that the metrics differ in their quantification of cross-lingual sharing in these models and suggest that the disagreement traces to anisotropy, the tendency of representations to cluster in a narrow cone of the embedding space. Only ILO's correlation with cross-lingual transfer (Spearman's $ρ= 0.90$) survives controls for model size, family, and per-task variation. We therefore recommend ILO as the primary sharing metric, to be reported alongside anisotropy diagnostics.

</details>


### [119] [Reinforcement Learning for improving Large Language Models' Catalan text simplification capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04823)
*Arnau Ayguadé Domingo,Stefan Bott,Horacio Saggion*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合SARI指标与特定惩罚项的新型奖励函数，利用群相对策略优化（GRPO）对IberianLLM-7B-Instruct进行强化学习后训练，显著提升了其在低资源语言（加泰罗尼亚语）上的自动文本简化（ATS）性能并抑制了负面行为，但跨语言迁移学习效果不显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动文本简化（ATS）对无障碍访问至关重要，但其发展速度滞后于更广泛的自然语言处理技术，特别是在低资源语言领域缺乏高质量的简化模型。

Method: 设计了一种新型奖励函数，将SARI评估指标与特定的惩罚组件相结合，用于指导大语言模型（LLMs）通过群相对策略优化（GRPO）进行强化学习；实验基于英语ASSET数据集对IberianLLM-7B-Instruct进行后训练，并探索了将ASSET翻译为加泰罗尼亚语和西班牙语后的跨语言迁移学习策略。

Result: 在英语ASSET数据集上后训练后，模型在两个定制的加泰罗尼亚语基准测试上的ATS性能得到提升，且成功抑制了先前观察到的负面行为；然而，通过在翻译后的ASSET数据集（加泰罗尼亚语和西班牙语版本）上进行后训练的跨语言迁移学习，并未在域外基准测试中显示出显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 利用定制奖励函数的GRPO强化学习方法能有效提升低资源语言的文本简化质量并修正模型行为，但简单的数据翻译策略在跨语言迁移学习中效果有限，未能显著改善域外泛化能力。

Abstract: Although automatic text simplification (ATS) is critical for accessibility, its progress has not matched the rapid evolution of broader natural language processing techniques. This paper investigates the application of reinforcement learning (RL) to improve the quality of ATS for low-resource languages using Large Language Models (LLMs). The paper introduces a novel reward function, designed to guide LLMs toward a targeted simplification style with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), that combines the SARI metric with specific penalty components. The effectiveness of GRPO with this reward function is motivated and demonstrated by post-training IberianLLM-7B-Instruct on the ASSET dataset. After post-training on the English ASSET, the model's ATS performance improves on two curated Catalan benchmarks while also successfully suppressing previously observed negative behaviors. Cross-lingual transfer learning is explored by translating ASSET into Catalan and Spanish and post-training the model on each version, but these fail to show a significant improvement on the out-of-domain benchmark.

</details>


### [120] [Generating Constructive Feedback on Stories via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04824)
*Maja Stahl,Timon Ziegenbein,Henning Wachsmuth*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种基于强化学习的方法，使LLM无需真实反馈数据即可生成针对创意写作的建设性反馈，通过多组件奖励函数优化反馈的针对性、改进性和关键性，在多个故事语料库上超越现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类专家反馈成本高、耗时长，而现有LLM生成的反馈往往泛化、缺乏可操作性且无法识别最关键写作问题，需要更高效且具建设性的自动反馈机制。

Method: 采用群体相对策略优化（GRPO）进行强化学习训练，设计包含针对性、故事质量改进和关键问题识别的多组件奖励函数，无需真实反馈数据即可引导LLM生成建设性反馈。

Result: 在三个故事语料库的自动与人工评估中，该方法优于包括Gemini在内的最先进LLM及竞争性基线模型，发现可操作性建议是反馈建设性的主要驱动因素。

Conclusion: 基于强化学习与多组件奖励函数的方法能有效提升LLM生成创意写作反馈的建设性，尤其通过提供可操作建议显著改善反馈质量，为自动写作助手提供可扩展解决方案。

Abstract: Constructive feedback is crucial for creative writers to refine their storytelling abilities. Since receiving feedback from human experts is often costly and time-intensive, large language models (LLMs) offer a scalable and efficient alternative as automatic writing assistants. Despite their potential, research indicates that LLM-generated feedback is often generic, lacks actionability, and fails to identify which writing issue is most critical. To address these limitations, we present a reinforcement learning approach that steers LLMs to generate constructive feedback without the need for ground-truth feedback. We train our model using group relative policy optimization (GRPO) with a novel multi-component reward function aiming at constructiveness: it prioritizes feedback that is uniquely tailored to the story, helps to improve story quality, and addresses the most critical writing issue. In automatic and human evaluation across three story corpora, our approach outperforms state-of-the-art LLMs (including Gemini) and competitive baselines. We find that providing actionable suggestions is the main driver of feedback constructiveness.

</details>


### [121] [On Epistemic Diversity in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04835)
*Elisabeth Kirsten,Nicole Krämer,Muhammad Bilal Zafar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将"认知多样性"作为大语言模型(LLMs)的新评估维度，指出当前LLMs倾向于将大量有效答案空间压缩为小的规范子集，存在认知狭隘问题，建议评估应超越准确性导向范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLMs评估主要关注准确性和对齐性，但在支持知识密集型任务时，系统可能给出正确答案却限制了用户获取替代性有效答案、解释或推理路径的机会，需要引入更全面的评估维度。

Method: 借鉴哲学和社会认识论中的认知多样性概念，将其形式化为LLMs向用户展示的有效答案、解释和推理路径的范围，提出概念化和测量LLMs认知多样性的初步框架，并在两个领域中进行操作化验证。

Result: 前沿LLMs常表现出认知狭隘性，反复将大型有效答案空间压缩为小的规范子集。

Conclusion: LLMs评估应超越以准确性为导向的范式，将认知多样性视为模型能力的重要维度。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used not only to retrieve information, but to answer questions, explain, teach, and support inquiry. In such settings, evaluation cannot be exhausted by accuracy or alignment alone. A system may give a correct answer while still narrowing users' access %to knowledge. to alternative valid answers, explanations, or reasoning routes. Drawing on the broader notion of epistemic diversity in philosophy and social epistemology, we formalize it in the context of LLMs as the range of valid answers, explanations, and reasoning routes that an LLM exposes to users. We argue that epistemic diversity is a useful evaluation dimension for settings where LLMs are used to support knowledge-intensive tasks. We propose a preliminary framework for conceptualizing and measuring epistemic diversity in LLMs, and operationalize it in two domains. We find that frontier LLMs often exhibit epistemic narrowness, repeatedly collapsing large valid answer spaces onto small canonical subsets. These findings suggest that LLM evaluation should move beyond accuracy-oriented paradigms and treat epistemic diversity as an important dimension of model capability.

</details>


### [122] [MABPD: Multi-Agent Bias Probing & Detection via Structured Argument Debate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04841)
*Garvit Joshi,Stavya Dhyani,Jasmine,Arun Chauhan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MABPD，一种无需训练的多智能体辩论框架，通过结构化论证在新闻偏见检测任务上达到了接近监督学习最先进模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决新闻偏见检测依赖大量标注数据进行监督训练的局限性，并应对单一模型难以捕捉微妙语言线索（如负载语言、选择性框架）的挑战。

Method: 引入MABPD流程，包含三个专用LLM智能体从互补视角分析文章，并通过结构化论证辩论（SAD）协议解决分歧。SAD采用领域驱动的不对称举证责任机制（无证据的偏见主张权重为零），结合角色加权投票和共识后验证，以显式辩论结构替代监督决策边界。

Result: 在BABE基准测试中，MABPD取得83.4%的宏平均F1值，仅比监督SOTA（MAGPIE, 84.1%）低0.7个百分点，且无需任务特定训练；在SemEval 2019跨数据集评估中实现75.0%的零样本准确率。消融实验显示，移除辩论模块会导致F1值下降高达10.6个点，证实了结构化 deliberation 的核心作用。

Conclusion: 结构化多智能体辩论可作为监督分类的原则性、无需训练的替代方案，在偏见检测任务中展现出卓越的泛化能力和性能，证明了显式推理结构优于单纯的智能体并行处理。

Abstract: Media bias in news articles operates through subtle linguistic cues---loaded language, selective framing, and strategic omission---that resist single-model detection and have traditionally required large annotated corpora for supervised training. We ask whether structured multi-agent deliberation can serve as a principled, training-free alternative to supervised classification for this task. We introduce MABPD (Multi-Agent Bias Probing & Detection), a pipeline in which three specialized LLM agents analyze an article from complementary perspectives and resolve disagreements through a Structured Argument Debate (SAD) protocol. SAD implements a domain-motivated asymmetric burden of proof---biased claims without grounded textual evidence carry zero weight---combined with role-weighted voting and post-consensus verification, replacing task-specific supervised decision boundaries with explicit deliberative structure. Ablation confirms that this structured deliberation, not mere agent parallelism, drives performance: removing the debate module reduces F1 by up to 10.6 points. On the BABE benchmark (4,121 expert-annotated sentences), MABPD achieves 83.4% macro F1 on the held-out test split---within 0.7 percentage points (pp) of the supervised SOTA (MAGPIE, 84.1% macro F1; Horych et al., 2024)---without any task-specific training or threshold tuning on annotated data. Cross-dataset evaluation on the SemEval 2019 HyperPartisan corpus (644 articles) yields 75.0% zero-shot accuracy, within 7.2 pp of the supervised SOTA accuracy (82.2%; Kiesel et al. 2019), confirming transfer across annotation regimes. We release the full pipeline and evaluation code.

</details>


### [123] [MMTClinic: Multimodal, Multilingual Time Series Question Answering and Reasoning Benchmark for Clinical Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04842)
*Sourav Malakar,Harshit Nigam,Akash Ghosh,Sriparna Saha,Amlan Chakrabarti,Saptarsi Goswami,Priti Singh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了MMTClinic基准，用于评估大型语言模型在临床时间序列数据上的多模态、多语言推理能力，涵盖30,000个问答对和5种语言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏能够反映真实临床场景复杂性的多模态、多语言且基于时间序列的基准，阻碍了临床可靠且语言包容性医疗AI系统的发展。

Method: 构建包含文本、医学图像和多变量生理信号的多模态数据集，设计涵盖死亡率预测、心率预测和SOFA评分估计三大临床任务的30,000个问答对（15,000道选择题和15,000道开放式问题），支持英语、印地语、孟加拉语、马拉地语和泰米尔语五种语言，并在零样本、少样本和思维链设置下评估13个最先进的大型语言模型。

Result: 评估结果显示模型在不同任务、语言和模态上的表现存在显著差异，揭示了当前模型在临床推理能力方面的局限性。

Conclusion: MMTClinic为推进多语言、多模态和时间序列感知的医疗AI研究提供了宝贵资源，数据集将在论文被接受后公开。

Abstract: Time-series data in clinical settings is crucial for capturing dynamic changes in a patient's health over time, enabling timely diagnosis, personalized treatment, and early detection of critical events. However, the development of clinically reliable and linguistically inclusive medical AI systems remains a significant challenge, primarily due to the lack of multimodal, multilingual, and time-series-grounded benchmarks that reflect the complexity of real-world clinical scenarios. To fill this gap, we present MMTClinic, a benchmark designed to evaluate large language models (LLMs) on complex reasoning and question-answering tasks involving clinical time-series. MMTClinic combines text, medical images, and multivariate physiological signals and includes 30,000 QA pairs (15,000 multiple choice questions (MCQs) and 15,000 open-ended questions) across five languages: English, Hindi, Bengali, Marathi, and Tamil. These questions cover three important clinical tasks---mortality prediction, heart rate forecasting, and SOFA score estimation. We evaluate 13 state-of-the-art LLMs in zero-shot, few-shot, and chain-of-thought settings. Our evaluation reveals notable differences in model performance across tasks, languages, and modalities, highlighting current limitations in clinical reasoning capabilities. MMTClinic provides a valuable resource for advancing multilingual, multimodal, and time-series-aware medical AI research. The dataset will be made publicly available on successful acceptance of the work.

</details>


### [124] [CC-Mediation: Evaluating Large Language Models for Cross-Cultural Conflict Mediation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04855)
*Suhyun Lee,Wenxuan Zhang,W. Quin Yow,Yang Deng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了CC-Mediation基准和基于DMIS的评估指标，揭示了当前大语言模型在跨文化调解中存在的干预时机判断偏差和策略生成崩溃问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决跨文化调解研究中缺乏具有可测量下游效应的调解数据集，以及缺乏评估跨文化立场变化的原则性指标这两个关键瓶颈。

Method: 构建了包含1,661个十轮对话的CC-Mediation基准，基于跨文化敏感度发展模型（DMIS）；提出了两种评估指标：衡量改善持久性的轨迹AUC（Trajectory AUC）和衡量立场转变幅度与方向的有符号Wasserstein-1距离。

Result: 新提出的指标与人类对DMIS立场转变的判断高度一致；实验发现LLM在干预时机上存在忽略对话内容的“位置先验”偏差，在调解策略上存在源于“深层提取崩溃”而非知识缺失的失效。

Conclusion: 当前大语言模型在跨文化调解的“何时干预”和“如何回应”两个维度上均存在局限性，分别归因于位置先验偏差和深层网络层的提取崩溃。

Abstract: Cross-cultural mediation by large language models (LLMs) requires deciding both when to intervene and how to respond in culturally grounded conflicts. Progress on this problem has been limited by the lack of (1) mediation datasets with measurable downstream effects and (2) principled metrics for evaluating intercultural stance change. To address these gaps, we introduce CC-Mediation, a cross-cultural mediation benchmark of $1{,}661$ ten-turn dialogues grounded in the Developmental Model of Intercultural Sensitivity (DMIS), containing culturally grounded conflicts, mediation interventions, and post-intervention trajectories. We further propose two DMIS-based evaluation metrics: Trajectory AUC, which measures the persistence of intercultural improvement over time, and a signed Wasserstein-1 distance, which measures the magnitude and direction of shifts in intercultural stance. Both metrics show strong agreement with human judgment of DMIS-grounded stance shift. Using CC-Mediation, we find that current LLMs have limitations on both axes: intervention timing (when) failure stems from a positional prior that ignores dialogue content, while mediation strategy (how) failure arises from a late-layer elicitation collapse rather than a knowledge deficit.

</details>


### [125] [Cache-Aware Joint Router Adaptation for Memory-Efficient MoE Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04895)
*Zhenhe Wu,Yaping Jin,Qinghua Xing,Hang Zhou,Wei He,Xianjie Wu,Xianfu Cheng,Jian Yang,Hanting Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种缓存感知的后训练框架，通过联合优化MoE主干网络和轻量级辅助缓存路由器，在保持推理时Top-K专家选择规则的同时，优化了专家缓存管理，显著提高了缓存命中率并减少了专家权重传输流量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型在推理时每次仅激活少量专家，但完整的专家集通常超出GPU显存，导致解码过程中需要反复传输权重，影响效率。

Method: 提出缓存感知的后训练框架，包含两种模式：1) Temporal Router：仅更新模式，预测同层复用并保留专家供未来token使用；2) Spatio-Temporal Router：在Temporal基础上增加Spatio Router，利用因果前驱的隐藏状态在访问目标层前优化时间缓存。

Result: 在Qwen3和GPT-OSS上评估，Temporal Router相比基线一致提高了缓存命中率并减少了流量；Spatio-Temporal Router在Qwen3上三个任务中实现了最佳负载调整效率，调整后命中率提升1.15-18.03点，流量减少4.6-53.3%。

Conclusion: 联合后训练比仅辅助训练产生更大改进，缓存容量控制传输需求，而优化预算决定了预访问覆盖率和主动传输之间的权衡。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models activate only a small subset of experts per token, but the full expert set often exceeds GPU memory, causing repeated weight transfers during decoding. We formulate expert-cache management as a model-side algorithmic problem and propose a cache-aware post-training framework that jointly adapts the MoE backbone and lightweight auxiliary cache routers while preserving the native Top-K expert-selection rule at inference. Its update-only mode, Temporal Router, predicts same-layer reuse and retains experts for future tokens without proactive loading. The full Spatio-Temporal Router adds a Spatio Router that uses the causal predecessor's hidden state to refine the temporal cache before target-layer access. We evaluate both modes on Qwen3 and GPT-OSS across GSM8K, MATH, and CommonsenseQA. Temporal Router consistently improves cache hit rate and reduces expert-weight traffic over matched LM-only baselines. On Qwen3, Spatio-Temporal Router achieves the best load-adjusted efficiency across three tasks, improving adjusted hit rate by 1.15--18.03 points and reducing traffic by 4.6--53.3% relative to the strongest evaluated prefetching baseline; results on GPT-OSS are competitive but task-dependent. An auxiliary-only ablation preserves baseline accuracy but yields modest cache gains, whereas joint post-training produces larger improvements. Sensitivity analyses show that cache capacity controls transfer demand, while the refinement budget governs the trade-off between pre-access coverage and proactive traffic.

</details>


### [126] [RefactorPlatform: An Open-Source Harness for Controlled Evaluation of Repository-Scale Refactoring Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04898)
*Aziz Ben Amor,Drish Mali,Mann Acharya,Vijayasri Iyer,Sébastien Bratières*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了RefactorPlatform，一个开源评估框架，用于在固定环境下隔离并评估代码代理在仓库级重构任务中的关键设计选择（如模型骨干、执行机制和提示特异性）。实验表明，AST感知的分块策略优于朴素分块，轻量级检索增强单代理优于多代理配置，且检索带来的准确性提升抵消了其代币成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仓库级重构要求代码代理在不改变程序行为的前提下，将单一变更传播到众多相互依赖的文件中。然而，目前缺乏能够隔离并确定影响代理在此类任务上成功与否的关键设计选择的评估框架。

Method: 开发RefactorPlatform开源评估框架，固定环境并显式变化三个设计轴：模型骨干（通过OpenRouter和GitHub Copilot CLI）、执行机制（基线、检索增强、多代理）和提示特异性。在隔离工作空间中运行，提供实时终端流、令牌/差异/转录日志、基于AST的验证及可导出遥测数据。在四个模型家族的100个多文件RefactorBench任务上进行演示评估。

Result: 1. AST感知分块在所有提示模式下比朴素令牌窗口分块性能提升25-30%；2. 朴素检索表现低于无检索基线；3. 轻量级检索增强单代理（成功率86%）优于评估的子代理配置（66%）；4. 检索带来的准确性提升吸收了其代币开销，使得每次成功重构的成本保持不变。

Conclusion: RefactorPlatform成功实现了对重构代理评估的可复现性和可审计性，并通过实证分析揭示了特定设计选择（如AST分块和检索增强单代理架构）对仓库级重构任务成功率的显著影响。

Abstract: Repository-scale refactoring requires coding agents to propagate a single change across many interdependent files without altering program behavior, yet to our knowledge no existing harness isolates the design choices that determine agent success on this task. We present RefactorPlatform, an open-source evaluation harness that holds the environment fixed and varies each design axis explicitly: model backbone (via OpenRouter and GitHub Copilot CLI), execution regime (baseline, retrieval-augmented, and multi-agent), and prompt specificity. Each run executes in an isolated workspace with live terminal streaming, per-task logging of tokens, diffs, and transcripts, AST-based verification, and exportable telemetry for audit and reproduction. Demonstrating the platform on 100 multi-file RefactorBench tasks across four model families, we illustrate the analyses it supports: AST-aware chunking outperforms naive token-window chunking by 25-30% across prompt modes, whereas naive retrieval falls below the retrieval-free baseline; a lean retrieval-augmented single agent (86%) beats the sub-agent configuration we evaluated (66%) on matched tasks with no task passing under delegation that fails under retrieval; and retrieval's accuracy gains absorb its token overhead, leaving cost per successful refactoring unchanged. RefactorPlatform is open-sourced to make refactoring-agent evaluation reproducible and auditable.

</details>


### [127] [Discourse Dependency: A Continuous Criterion for Translation Difficulty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04959)
*Ahrii Kim,Chanjun Park,Seong-heum Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出话语依赖（DDP）作为衡量机器翻译中长距离上下文需求的新指标，发现现有基准测试偏向低DDP片段，且随着DDP增加，现有上下文注入策略无法跟上人工后编辑水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器翻译基准测试缺乏对"难度"的明确定义，特别是未衡量翻译片段需要回溯文档多远距离才能解析实体和代词这一关键维度。

Method: 提出话语依赖（DDP）指标，通过命名实体重提及和代词共指计算源端度量，无需依赖外部指标；在WMT24++和WMT25上应用DDP分析，并比较五种上下文注入策略在英韩后编辑任务中的表现。

Result: DDP在99.2%片段中单向误差，高DDP片段被认证需要长距离上下文；现有基准测试严重偏向低DDP片段；当DDP>=15时，评分者偏好人工翻译，但自动指标未显示差异。

Conclusion: DDP将评估焦点从模型得分高低转移到模型上下文覆盖范围，揭示了当前机器翻译系统在长距离话语依赖处理上的局限性。

Abstract: Recent calls for harder machine translation benchmarks have not clarified what difficulty should mean. We argue that one meaningful and currently unmeasured axis is referential reach, the distance a segment must look back into its document to resolve the entities and pronouns it contains. We formalize this as discourse dependency (DDP), a metric-free, source-side measure computed from named entity re-mentions and pronominal coreference. Validated against gold coreference, DDP errs one-sidedly in 99.2% of segments, so a high-DDP segment is certified to require long-range context. Applying DDP to WMT24++ and WMT25 shows that both are heavily skewed toward low-DDP segments, which domain labels do not distinguish. Building on DDP, we compare five context injection strategies in an English-Korean post-editing setup, varying context size and selection. As DDP grows, no strategy keeps pace with human post-editing. On segments with DDP >= 15 raters prefer human translations, while automatic metrics register no difference. As frontier systems saturate aggregate scores, DDP shifts evaluation from how well models score to how far they can reach.

</details>


### [128] [BIT.UA at BioASQ 14B: Modular Retrieval with pg_textsearch and Qdrant, and Agent-Based Answer Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04999)
*André Ribeiro,Rúben Garrido,Alexander Christiansen,Richard A. A. Jonker,Sérgio Matos*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BIT.UA 团队在 BioASQ 第 14 届任务 B 中，通过重构代码库、升级检索架构（pg_textsearch + Qdrant）及引入 LLM 裁判与代理共识机制，在生物医学问答挑战中取得了具有竞争力的结果（Phase A MAP 排名第 5）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在提升生物医学问答系统的性能与效率，解决以往架构在检索效率和生成质量上的局限，并探索多代理协作与新型检索策略在复杂 biomedical 领域的应用潜力。

Method: 1. 检索阶段：将 PyTerrier PISA 替换为基于 PostgreSQL 的 pg_textsearch 进行 BM25 检索，采用 Qdrant 进行稠密向量索引以支持 GPU 加速；探索 HyDE 查询扩展与 Context-1 策略；开发包含稠密检索负采样的新重排序训练流程。2. 生成阶段：引入“LLM 即裁判”框架及 novel 代理共识机制（多代理辩论并迭代收敛）；首次参与片段生成子任务。

Result: 系统在所有批次中均取得竞争性结果，其中 Phase A 系统在第 1 和第 3 批次的 MAP 排名达到第 5 位；成功实现了架构模块化与检索生成组件的显著优化。

Conclusion: 架构重构与多代理共识机制显著提升了系统性能；未来工作将聚焦于整合 SPLADE 和 ColBERT 等先进检索模型以进一步优化效果。

Abstract: This paper describes the participation of the BIT.UA team from the University of Aveiro in the 14th edition of the BioASQ Task B challenge on biomedical question answering. Building on our previous submissions, we introduced a substantially refactored and modular codebase, and made significant changes to both the retrieval and generation components of the pipeline. For Phase~A document retrieval, we replaced the PyTerrier PISA index with PostgreSQL-based pg\_textsearch for BM25 retrieval and adopted Qdrant for dense embedding indexing, enabling more efficient storage and GPU-accelerated similarity search. We explored HyDE-based query expansion alongside a Context-1 retrieval strategy. A new reranker training pipeline was developed, incorporating dense retrieval for negative sampling. For Phases A+ and B answer generation, we introduced an LLM-as-a-judge framework and a novel agent quorum mechanism, where multiple agents with diverse prompts debate and iteratively converge on a consensus answer using adaptive document retention. We also participated in the snippets generation subtask for the first time. Our systems achieved competitive results across all batches, with Phase~A systems achieving MAP ranks of 5 (Batch~1,3). We discuss the impact of these architectural changes, lessons learned, and outline directions for future work including SPLADE and ColBERT integration. All code is openly available: https://github.com/bioinformatics-ua/BioASQ14b.

</details>


### [129] [MoirfEolas and CríochScore: Developing Resources for and the Evaluation of Tokenization Alignment with Irish Morphology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05022)
*Jane Adkins,Abigail Walsh,Brian Davis,Elaine Uí Dhonnchadha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了爱尔兰语的新分词资源MoirfEolas数据集和评估指标CríochScore，发现Unigram语言模型在形态对齐上表现最佳，并揭示了分词形态对齐与压缩率及词汇效率之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 爱尔兰语属于低资源语言，缺乏专门的分词资源和形态边界对齐评估方法，需要开发针对性的NLP工具以提升其处理能力。

Method: 构建包含35,000+爱尔兰语词汇及其元音变换、前缀和后缀映射的MoirfEolas数据集，设计CríochScore指标评估分词与形态边界的对齐程度，并对比多种分词算法的性能。

Result: Unigram语言模型在形态对齐上优于其他算法；分词的形态对齐与压缩率、词汇效率之间存在权衡关系。

Conclusion: 该研究为爱尔兰语NLP开发提供了实用见解，其数据集和方法可推广至其他低资源语言，助力构建专用形态资源。

Abstract: This paper presents new tokenization resources for Irish and evaluation measures of alignment with the morphological boundaries of the language. We present MoirfEolas, a dataset of over 35,000 Irish words mapped to their respective eclipses, prefixes and suffixes as well as an evaluation metric CríochScore, that evaluates the alignment of tokenizations with the morphological boundaries present in MoirfEolas. We evaluate common tokenization algorithms using CríochScore as well as intrinsic metrics present in the tokenization literature. We find that the Unigram Language Model aligns with Irish morphology more often than the other algorithms evaluated. We also find trade-offs between morphological-alignment of tokenization with both compression as well as vocabulary efficiency, providing practical insights for Irish natural language processing development. This dataset contributes towards combating the Irish language's low-resource status; moreover, the construction process reported in this paper can be emulated by other languages to create specialised morphological resources.

</details>


### [130] [Leveraging Low-Level Symbolic Competences for Unsupervised Grounding in Hallucination Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05025)
*Renato Vukovic,Hsien-chin Lin,Carel van Niekerk,Benjamin Ruppik,Michael Heck,Shutong Feng,Nurul Lubis,Milica Gasic*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用LLM的SQL能力构建数据库，通过神经符号方法在无监督情况下检测大模型幻觉的新方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的幻觉问题（生成事实错误或无依据的内容）是主要挑战，由于LLM推理过程不透明，难以检测其输出为何不准确。

Method: 让LLM从参考文档构建SQL数据库，利用该数据库对参考内容和采样响应进行推理，构建基于数据库的幻觉检测流程，提供神经符号检查。

Result: 在RAGTruth和DiaHalu数据集上，该方法优于直接预测，可与最先进的幻觉检测方法竞争，且无需领域特定的微调。

Conclusion: 利用LLM已有的低级通用能力（如SQL）进行神经符号方法是有效的，值得进一步研究低级LLM能力在神经符号方法中的应用。

Abstract: Hallucination-where a language model generates outputs that are factually incorrect or unsupported by the source-is a major challenge for both prompted and fine-tuned language models. Detecting hallucinations is difficult due to the opaque reasoning processes of LLMs, which often provide little insight into why a model's output may be inaccurate.
  In this work, we investigate whether an LLM can use an alternative, low level, symbolic competence such as SQL for unsupervised hallucination detection in some high level task. For this, we make an LLM build an SQL database from reference documents. This SQL database is then used for reasoning over the reference and the sampled response in a hallucination detection pipeline that is grounded in the database, thereby providing a neurosymbolic checkup.
  On RAGTruth and DiaHalu hallucination detection datasets, we find that our approach improves on direct prediction and competes with state-of-the-art hallucination detection methods, while not requiring domain-specific fine-tuning. Instead it relies on a low-level general competence already present in LLMs. This warrants further investigation of low-level LLM competences in neurosymbolic approaches.

</details>


### [131] [How do LLMs Evaluate Perceived Moral Agency? Investigating Moral Decision-Making in Human-Artificial Agents Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05037)
*Fernanda Mansilla,Aloysius Tok,Bahia Guellaï,Farah Benamara,Nancy F. Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次通过实证研究比较了人类与大型语言模型（LLMs）在智慧城市场景中对人类与自主人工代理（AAs）的感知道德主体性（PMA）评估，发现人类对AAs的道德主体性评价低于人类，而LLMs在道德困境中更关注情境严重性而非代理本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLMs承担需要道德建议的角色，以及人工代理（如机器人、无人机等）日益嵌入智慧城市环境，理解人类与LLMs如何归因道德主体性变得至关重要。

Method: 采用经过验证的感知道德主体性（PMA）量表的改编版本，对190名人类参与者及多种LLMs进行实验，在智慧城市场景中对人类与不同具身形态的自主人工代理进行PMA评估比较。

Result: 人类对AAs的感知道德主体性低于人类；LLMs在面对具体道德困境时，优先考量伤害严重程度与情境紧迫性，而非对代理本身的稳定评估，表现出与人类评分者相似的情境敏感性。

Conclusion: 随着LLMs日益参与日常道德决策，理解其对道德主体性的归因方式具有重要意义，研究发现LLMs在道德推理中表现出高度情境依赖性。

Abstract: As LLMs take on roles requiring moral advice, understanding how they attribute moral agency becomes critical. Humans possess moral agency, the capacity to make ethically guided decisions and bear responsibility for their consequences, a well-established construct in moral psychology. Yet as artificial agents (AAs) such as robots, drones, and disembodied AI systems become increasingly embedded in smart city environments, the question of whether and how moral agency is attributed to them takes on new urgency. This paper presents, to the best of our knowledge, the first empirical study comparing how humans and LLMs evaluate perceived moral agency (PMA) across human and autonomous artificial agents varying in embodiment, situated in plausible smart city scenarios. Using an adaptation of a validated PMA scale, we applied a protocol to 190 human participants as well as various LLMs. Our evaluation reveals higher perceptions of moral agency in humans than in AAs. However, when facing moral dilemmas in concrete scenarios, LLMs reason outward from the situation, prioritizing harm severity and contextual urgency over any stable assessment of the agent itself, amplifying a context-sensitivity also present in human raters. These findings are particularly relevant as LLMs become increasingly involved in everyday moral decisions.

</details>


### [132] [EuroAlpaca: Task-Preserving Localisation of Instruction Data for European Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05043)
*Aleix Sant,Jordi Luque,Carlos Escolano*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出EuroAlpaca，一种任务保留的本地化管道，用于多语言指令微调，解决了直接机器翻译导致任务关键约束失真的问题，并在50种欧洲语言上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器翻译可扩展英语指令微调数据至多语言，但会扭曲任务关键约束和输出要求，导致训练数据损坏和模型性能下降。

Method: 提出EuroAlpaca管道：根据示例类型，采用字段级机器翻译保留任务关键内容，或重建任务等价的目标语言实例，随后验证跨字段一致性和目标语言一致性；同时构建European-IFEval多语言基准用于可验证的指令遵循评估。

Result: 在四个LLM的LoRA实验中，直接翻译数据在Aya评估套件上提升ROUGE-L和F-BERT，但在European-IFEval上准确率相对未适应基线下降29.8%；而EuroAlpaca适应方法使准确率提升12.9%，逆转了直接MT的退化，同时在Aya上获得最高ROUGE-L和F-BERT分数。

Conclusion: 保留任务语义对于多语言指令微调至关重要，EuroAlpaca有效解决了直接机器翻译导致的任务失真问题。

Abstract: Machine translation (MT) offers a scalable way to extend English instruction-tuning data to multiple languages, but it can distort task-critical constraints and required outputs, creating corrupted training examples and degrading models trained on such data. We introduce EuroAlpaca, a task-preserving localisation pipeline and near-parallel resource covering 50 European languages and regional varieties, together with European-IFEval, a multilingual benchmark for verifiable instruction following. Depending on the example, our pipeline applies field-wise MT while preserving task-critical content or reconstructs a task-equivalent target-language instance, followed by validation of cross-field coherence and target-language consistency. Across LoRA experiments with four LLMs, training on directly translated data improves ROUGE-L and F-BERT on the Aya Evaluation Suite, but reduces accuracy on European-IFEval by 29.8% relative to the unadapted baseline. In contrast, adaptation with EuroAlpaca improves accuracy by 12.9% over the same baseline, reversing the degradation caused by direct MT, while also achieving the highest ROUGE-L and F-BERT scores on Aya. These results show that preserving task semantics is essential for multilingual instruction tuning.

</details>


### [133] [A Structured Debate-Mixture-of-Agents Framework for Complex Clinical Diagnostic Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05069)
*Chang Xia,Leilei Ouyang,Huimin Wang,Yong Zhao,Kang Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DMoA 是一个基于角色交互的多智能体框架，用于提升大语言模型在复杂医疗诊断中的准确性和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLMs）在医疗任务中展现出潜力，但其单轮问答形式无法反映临床诊断的实际流程，导致在复杂诊断场景中存在局限性。

Method: 开发了 Debate-Mixture-of-Agents (DMoA) 框架，通过结构化的基于角色的交互支持迭代式诊断推理，并在 297 个罕见病案例和 1,719 个挑战性案例上进行评估。

Result: DMoA 在两个数据集上均显著优于 GPT-4o 基线，最可能诊断准确率提升了 10.21 个百分点，安全率提升了 11.36 个百分点；消融实验表明性能提升源于结构化工作流而非仅靠增加模型数量或输出长度。

Conclusion: DMoA 展示了多智能体框架在临床任务中的潜力，建议进一步研究该框架的设计、基座模型选择及 token 预算对性能的影响。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) show potential for medical tasks, but their single-turn question-answer format does not reflect how clinical diagnosis is performed in practice. As a result, they remain limited in complex diagnostic settings. We developed Debate-Mixture-of-Agents (DMoA), a novel multi-agent framework that structures role-based interaction to support iterative diagnostic reasoning. Base models and DMoA were evaluated on 297 rare disease cases and 1,719 challenging cases. Across both datasets, DMoA improved most likely diagnosis accuracy by 10.21 percentage points and safety rate by 11.36 percentage points over GPT-4o baseline. Ablation experiments showed that the gains were not simply due to the use of more models or longer outputs, but also reflected the contribution of the structured workflow. Further analyses examined how framework design, base model choice, and token budget affected performance. DMoA performed better with a 4*2 structure, stronger base models, and a larger token budget. These findings demonstrate the potential of DMoA for clinical tasks and suggest further investigation of multi-agent frameworks.

</details>


### [134] [Influence Score and Transformers interpretability: Measure of the Effective Impact of Attention Heads at inference time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05074)
*Lisa Bouger,Yannick Teglia,Philippe Loubet Moundi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种影响力评分方法，用于量化注意力头在基于 Transformer 的提示注入检测模型分类决策中的贡献，揭示了正确与错误预测间的决策行为差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在细粒度的电路分析与全局基于输出的方法之间取得平衡，并系统地研究 Transformer 分类器的决策机制，特别是针对提示注入检测任务。

Method: 提出一种结合了对 logits 的方向性影响和在残差流中结构性贡献的影响力评分，实现了在注意力头、层和网络层面的多尺度分析，并将其应用于专门用于提示注入检测的 DeBERTa 模型。

Result: 该框架揭示了模型在正确预测和错误预测之间表现出截然不同的决策行为。

Conclusion: 该方法为研究 Transformer 分类器的决策机制提供了一种有效的折衷方案和系统化途径。

Abstract: We propose an influence score to quantify the contribution of attention heads to classification decisions in Transformer-based models designed for prompt injection detection. The score combines directional influence on the logits with structural contribution within the residual stream, enabling a multi-scale analysis at the head, layer, and network levels. Applied to a DeBERTa model specialized for prompt injection detection, our framework reveals distinct decision behaviours between correct and erroneous predictions. Our method provides an effective compromise between fine-grained circuit analysis and global output-based methods, and offers a systematic way to study decision mechanisms in Transformer classifiers.

</details>


### [135] [Improving Language Identification for Code-Switched Utterances with Integer Linear Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05099)
*Joanna Radoła,Josep Maria Crego,François Yvon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了MaskLID方法，解决了其对词级语言关联分数的过度依赖问题，并将其优化算法重新表述为整数线性规划，通过添加可解释的约束条件显著提升了代码混合语言识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码混合（CS）语句的自动识别对语言识别（LID）系统仍是一个挑战，导致此类文本在大型语言模型的训练数据中代表性不足。

Method: 1. 揭示并解决MaskLID过度依赖词级语言关联分数的问题；2. 将底层优化算法重新表述为整数线性规划（ILP）；3. 引入大量清晰且可解释的约束条件进行实验。

Result: 在涉及10种多样化语言的实验中，改进后的系统在代码混合基准测试上表现出显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过对MaskLID的改进，包括优化算法重构和约束条件引入，大幅提升了基线系统的代码混合识别能力，并发布了代码和数据以保证可复现性。

Abstract: Automatic identification of code-switched (CS) utterances remains a challenge for language identification (LID) systems, causing such texts to be underrepresented in the training data of Large Language Models. In this paper, we revisit MaskLID, a state-of-the art approach for CS identification, which requires no training and detects arbitrary language combinations. We make three main contributions: (a) we reveal, and address, a major issue of MaskLID: its overreliance on word-level language association scores; (b) we reformulate the underlying optimization algorithm as an Integer Linear Program, enabling us to experiment with a large set of clear and interpretable constraints; (c) each of these improvements vastly improves the baseline system, as we illustrate in experiments involving 10~diverse languages, where we observe a strong boost in performance on CS benchmarks. We release our code and data for reproducibility.

</details>


### [136] [NS-ST-GraphRAG: Neuro-Symbolic Spatio-Temporal GraphRAG for Literary Knowledge Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05139)
*Zheng Kui Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出NS-ST-GraphRAG框架，通过整合本体引导提取、时空约束及动态子图检索，解决长篇小说叙事中证据分散及关系演变的检索增强生成（RAG）难题，并在首个古典中文文学多跳问答基准Red-Chamber-QA上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长篇小说叙事对检索增强生成构成独特挑战：相关证据跨章节分布，人物与事件关系随叙事时间演变，且正确答案往往依赖于时间、空间及关系约束的联合推理，传统单一语料库图谱检索难以应对。

Method: 提出NS-ST-GraphRAG（神经符号时空图谱RAG）框架，核心组件包括：本体引导的信息提取、确定性约束检查、双重时间坐标系统、空间场景属性建模以及动态子图检索机制；同时构建Red-Chamber-QA基准，包含时间、空间及通用问题类别，提供每部分证据跨度及确定性捷径控制。

Result: 在120题保留测试集上，NS-ST-GraphRAG的机械答案复现率为0.733（优于冻结窗口基线的0.675和闭卷模型的0.083，但统计显著性未达标p=0.092）；语义评判准确率为0.866（优于基线的0.850）。预设的H2约束类别条件未获数据支持。

Conclusion: 研究证明了将时序图谱表示、约束性提取与可审计评估整合为统一框架的可行性，有效实现了长篇小说叙事中可验证的知识处理。

Abstract: Long-form literary narratives pose a distinctive information-processing challenge for retrieval-augmented generation: relevant evidence is distributed across chapters, relations evolve over narrative time, and correct answers may depend jointly on temporal, spatial, and relational constraints. We propose NS-ST-GraphRAG, a neuro-symbolic spatio-temporal GraphRAG framework that integrates ontology-guided extraction, deterministic constraint checking, dual temporal coordinates, spatial scene attributes, and dynamic sub-graph retrieval. Instead of retrieving from a single corpus-level graph, the framework selects the graph state valid for the temporal and spatial scope of a query and grounds generated answers in traceable evidence. We further introduce Red-Chamber-QA, to our knowledge the first open multi-hop question-answering benchmark for classical Chinese literature, with time-, space-, and general-question categories, per-part evidence spans, and deterministic shortcut controls. On a 120-question held-out split, NS-ST-GraphRAG achieves mechanical answer reproduction of 0.733 versus 0.675 for the frozen window baseline and 0.083 for a closed-book model (McNemar exact p = 0.092, directionally favorable but not significant); semantic-judge accuracy is 0.866 versus 0.850. The pre-specified constrained-category condition of H2 is not supported by the delivered comparison. These results show how temporal graph representation, constrained extraction, and auditable evaluation integrate into a unified framework for verifiable knowledge processing over long-form narrative.

</details>


### [137] [A Human-in-the-Loop Framework for AI-Assisted Scoring in Large-Scale Writing Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05143)
*María Eugenia Curi,Germán Capdehourat,Isabel Amigo,Magdalena Romano,Rosana Serra,Adrián Silveira,Andrés Peri*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究设计并验证了一种用于大规模国家评估中短文评分的AI辅助框架，采用“人在回路”策略，结果显示AI与人工评分在多数维度上具有中高度一致性，能有效优化专家资源分配，但强调必须在精心设计的人工监督下才能安全整合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用人工智能（特别是大语言模型）提升教育评估中评分过程的效率与可扩展性，同时解决大规模评估中人工工作量过大的问题。

Method: 基于两次全国性考试（每次约5000份学生答卷）的真实操作数据，设计包含“人在回路”策略的AI辅助评分框架，针对150-200字的短文，分析AI评分与人工评分在多个量表维度上的一致性，并评估该决策流程对通过/失败结果的影响。

Result: 模型与人工评估在大多数维度上显示出中度到高度的一致性；提出的校正工作流程能精准识别需要人工复核的案例，实现了专家精力的高效分配。

Conclusion: AI辅助评分只有在结合精心设计的人工监督时，才能安全地整合到大规模评估过程中；未来需关注模型与人工一致性的纵向监测及AI支持工作流可能引入的认知偏差。

Abstract: The integration of artificial intelligence (AI), particularly large language models (LLMs), into educational assessment has opened new opportunities to enhance the efficiency and scalability of grading processes. This study presents the design and validation of an AI-assisted scoring framework for written responses in a large-scale national assessment. The proposed approach focuses on short written texts of approximately 150-200 words and incorporates a human-in-the-loop strategy to preserve assessment quality while reducing manual workload. The study is grounded in a real operational context, using data from two recent editions of a nationwide test, each comprising approximately 5,000 student responses. We analyze the alignment between AI-generated scores and human raters across multiple rubric dimensions, as well as the impact of the proposed decision flow on pass/fail outcomes. Results show moderate to high agreement between the model and human evaluations in most dimensions, supporting the feasibility of AI assistance in this setting. Moreover, the proposed correction workflow identifies cases where human review is most valuable, enabling a more efficient allocation of expert effort. The findings suggest that AI-assisted scoring can be safely integrated into large-scale assessment processes only when combined with carefully designed human oversight. The paper concludes by discussing practical implications for deployment in national assessment systems and outlining future research directions, including longitudinal monitoring of model-human alignment and the analysis of potential cognitive bias introduced by AI-supported review workflows.

</details>


### [138] [From Vision to Language: Investigating Causal Information Flow in Multimodal Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05149)
*Davide Testa,Hugh Mee Wong,Alessandro Lenci,Bernardo Magnini,Albert Gatt*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过分层因果干预分析视频 - 语言模型（VLM）中的跨模态信息流，发现视觉信息主要在模型处理候选答案时整合，名词和动词分别作为语义锚点和时序关系处理的关键，同时指出 VLM 在重构视频帧序列信息方面存在困难，这可能源于语言偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉 - 语言模型评估仅关注最终预测结果，缺乏对决策是否基于视觉证据的深入理解，因此需要追踪视觉信息如何贡献于语言决策，以揭示跨模态信息流机制。

Method: 在基于视频生成的多选场景中，对视频 - 文本注意力路径应用分层因果干预，针对空间、因果和时序视觉推理进行分析。

Result: 视觉信息主要在模型处理候选答案选项时整合，这些选项是最终决策的主要文本依据；名词在多模态增强中充当语义锚点，动词在处理时序关系时更为关键；VLM 在跨视频帧重构序列信息方面表现脆弱，且这种脆弱性可能与定义场景事件关系的特定时间表达的语言偏差有关。

Conclusion: VLM 的决策依赖于候选答案阶段的视觉信息整合，词性（名词/动词）在多模态推理中扮演不同角色，且模型在时序推理上的局限性可能部分源于语言偏差而非纯粹的视觉理解缺陷。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models are commonly evaluated through their final predictions, but understanding whether these decisions are grounded in visual evidence requires tracing how visual information contributes to language-based decisions. With this purpose in mind, we investigate cross-modal information flow in a video-based generative multiple-choice-like setting by applying a layer-wise causal intervention on video-text attention pathways. We target spatial, causal, and temporal visual reasoning. Our results show that visual information is mainly integrated while the model processes the candidate answer options, which serve as the primary textual grounding sites for the final decision. We further show that nouns play an important role as semantic anchors during multimodal enrichment, while verbs are more relevant when temporal relations are processed. Finally, we identify a distinct pattern in temporal reasoning, suggesting that VLMs struggle to reconstruct sequential information across video frames, but we remark that such fragility may also reflect linguistic biases associated with specific temporal expressions used for defining the relation between events within a scene.

</details>


### [139] [Compression Beyond the Uncompressed: A Two-Stage Training Recipe for Soft Context Compression in RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05152)
*Shuyu Guo,Shuo Zhang,Zhaochun Ren*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DEX-Comp通过两阶段训练方法（纯蒸馏预热和硬探索强化学习）实现16倍上下文压缩和4-24倍推理加速，在保持或超越未压缩RAG性能的同时显著提升效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软上下文压缩方法通过蒸馏未压缩RAG系统输出进行训练，导致性能受限于原始模型，无法充分发挥压缩表示的潜力。

Method: 提出两阶段训练方案：第一阶段纯蒸馏仅在未压缩RAG正确响应上预热压缩模型；第二阶段硬探索对未压缩RAG失败的查询进行强化学习，探索更适合压缩表示的计算模式。

Result: 在5个开放域QA基准测试中，实现16倍上下文压缩和4-24倍推理加速，在top-5到top-30检索深度下性能达到或超过未压缩RAG基线。

Conclusion: 消融实验和跨数据集、跨骨干网络评估验证了各阶段贡献及方法泛化性，证明DEX-Comp能有效解决压缩RAG性能受限问题。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) enhances language models with external knowledge, but the lengthy retrieved context inflates the input and degrades inference efficiency. Soft context compression encodes each document into a substantially shorter embedding sequence. However, most existing approaches are trained by distilling outputs from uncompressed RAG systems, inherently limiting their performance relative to the original model. To address this limitation, we propose DEX-Comp, a two-stage training recipe: Pure Distillation warm-starts the compression model on the uncompressed RAG's correct responses only, and Hard Exploration then runs reinforcement learning solely on queries the uncompressed RAG fails, forcing the model to explore computation patterns better suited to compressed representations. On five open-domain QA benchmarks at retrieval depths from top-5 to top-30, DEX-Comp compresses retrieved contexts by $16\times$ and accelerates inference by $4\times$--$24\times$, while achieving performance comparable to or exceeding the uncompressed RAG baseline across retrieval depths. Ablations and evaluations across diverse datasets and backbones further confirm the contribution of each stage and the generalization of our approach.

</details>


### [140] [Can Large Language Models Anticipate Behavioral Responses to Social Policies? A Case of Pension Enrollment Prediction among China's Flexible Workers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05189)
*Yumiao Li,Peixin Liu,Donglin Di,Chen Li,Runhuan Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出FlexPension-LLM，首个针对中国灵活就业人员养老金参保预测的领域专用大语言模型，通过注入政策依据提示和误差过滤监督，在性能上超越多数基线及教师模型，为政策评估提供低成本、高可靠的新工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统计量经济学方法在推演假设性政策场景时可靠性不足，而实地试点项目成本高昂，亟需一种高效、低成本且可解释的政策影响评估工具。

Method: 提出DKI-RDistill方法：1）在提示中注入基于Probit边际效应和户籍-省份养老金规则的政策依据线索；2）利用LoRA/SFT将带有推理理由的监督信号蒸馏到开放权重的MoE学生模型；3）通过基于真实标签再生成教师模型错误案例进行误差修正。

Result: 在CHFS 2019盲测集上，FlexPension-LLM复合F1得分为0.9316，优于Claude Sonnet 4.5教师模型及17个基线中的15个，性能与Claude Opus 4.6无统计显著差异；在四个外部调查数据集上平均复合F1为0.7549，且表现波动最小。

Conclusion: 性能提升主要源于政策依据线索注入和误差过滤监督机制；生成的推理理由可作为决策轨迹，便于对照政策规则进行验证，表明LLM经领域适配后可成为可靠的政策评估辅助工具。

Abstract: Assessing the impacts of social policy changes is a widely acknowledged challenge for policymakers. Econometric methods can be unreliable when extrapolating to hypothetical scenarios, while field pilot programs are highly costly. In this paper, we propose using large language models (LLMs) as policy-assessment tools adapted from general-purpose models. We present FlexPension-LLM, the first domain-specialized large language model for a hierarchical pension-enrollment prediction task among flexible workers in China, and introduce DKI-RDistill, which injects policy-grounded cues into the prompt, including Probit-derived marginal effects and hukou-province pension rules. The method then uses LoRA/SFT to distill rationale-augmented supervision into an open-weight MoE student, with teacher errors corrected by regenerating those cases under ground-truth labels. On a CHFS 2019 blind split, FlexPension-LLM achieves 0.9316 Composite F1, surpassing its Claude Sonnet 4.5 teacher and 15 of 17 baselines, and is statistically indistinguishable from Claude Opus 4.6. Across four external surveys, it averages 0.7549 Composite F1 and shows the narrowest performance range among the strongest systems. Component analysis shows that gains come mainly from policy-grounded cue injection and error-filtered supervision, while rationales provide decision traces that can be checked against policy rules.

</details>


### [141] [A Verifier-Guided Explainable Reasoning Framework with Gold-Anchored QLoRA, Task-Aware Mixture-of-Experts, and Group-Relative RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05221)
*Thi Kim Trang Vo,Nam Tien Le,Thi Kim Nguyet Vo,Minh Khang Tran,Duy Phuong Tran*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于教育问答的验证器引导的可解释推理框架，结合QLoRA、符号路由和RLVR，通过验证器反馈提升推理深度和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLMs）虽具备强大推理能力，但其解释往往不一致、缺乏依据或难以验证，需要提升透明度和可验证性。

Method: 采用gold-anchored QLoRA微调Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct，结合任务感知符号路由（逻辑问题用FOL/Z3验证器，物理问题用公式/单位感知求解器），并通过RLVR利用验证器反馈进行候选评估、自修正和奖励构建，推理阶段采用无gold自一致性聚合。

Result: 在438个测试样本上，RLVR将P3（推理深度与可解释性）从50.68%提升至72.20%，混合P1（答案正确性）稳定在55.94%；自一致性将仅模型P1从48.86%提升至50.23%，符号验证提供额外系统级增益。

Conclusion: RLVR主要增强显式推理结构，符号验证在系统层面补充神经策略，提升答案可靠性，整体框架有效平衡了推理可解释性与答案准确性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) show strong reasoning ability, but their explanations can remain inconsistent, weakly grounded, or difficult to verify. We propose a verifier-guided explainable reasoning framework for transparent educational question answering that combines gold-anchored QLoRA, task-aware symbolic routing, and group-relative RLVR. Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct is first adapted with field-weighted QLoRA supervision anchored to authoritative answers. A lightweight router then assigns logic problems to a FOL/Z3 verifier and physics problems to a formula- and unit aware symbolic solver. Verifier feedback is further used to support candidate evaluation, self-revision, and reward construction during RLVR. Candidate responses are evaluated along three complementary dimensions: P1 for answer correctness, P2 for evidence or unit consistency, and P3 for reasoning depth and explainability. At inference, gold-free self-consistency aggregates multiple candidate responses before an optional question-only physics verifier performs conservative system-level correction. On 438 held-out examples, RLVR increases P3 from 50.68% to 72.20%, while hybrid P1 remains approximately stable at 55.94%. Self-consistency improves model only P1 from 48.86% to 50.23%, with symbolic verification providing the remaining hybrid gain. These results indicate that RLVR primarily strengthens explicit reasoning structure, while symbolic verification complements the neural policy by improving answer reliability at the system level.

</details>


### [142] [Self-Supervised Lexical Representation Learning for Fast, Large-Scale Phylogenetic Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05262)
*Tim Wientzek*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种完全自监督的对比学习框架，可直接从原始IPA转写的词表学习词汇表示，无需同源注释、对齐或专家输入，用于大规模系统发育推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算系统发育学在历史语言学中至关重要，但其全球规模应用受限于两个因素：基于字符的方法需要劳动密集型的同源判断手动注释，以及大型数据集推断的计算成本高昂。

Method: 采用双重对比目标：词级损失将语音相似形式组织到连贯空间中，辅助语言级损失鼓励词汇空间反映语言更广泛的音系特性。从所得词表示中推导成对语言距离，用于推断包含3,399种语言变体的全球系统发育树。

Result: 推断的树与Glottolog参考树的广义四重距离（GQD）具有竞争力，仅需在标准笔记本GPU上计算数分钟。相同表示捕捉历时概念稳定性，方差产生的稳定性排名与既定排名显著相关。消融研究证实语言级目标和语音特征向量使用改善了推断树的拓扑结构。

Conclusion: 该框架为大规模系统发育推断提供了计算高效且完全自动的替代方案，并提供了支持语言和概念级别下游分析的统一表示。

Abstract: Computational phylogenetics has become an essential tool in historical linguistics, yet its application at a global scale remains constrained by two factors: the labor-intensive manual annotation of cognacy judgments required for character-based methods and the substantial computational cost of inference on large datasets. This paper introduces a fully self-supervised contrastive learning framework that learns lexical representations directly from raw IPA-transcribed wordlists, without requiring cognacy annotations, alignments, or additional expert input. The model employs a dual contrastive objective: a word-level loss that organizes phonetically similar forms into a coherent space, and an auxiliary language-level loss that encourages the lexical space to reflect broader phonological properties of languages. From the resulting word representations, pairwise language distances are derived and used to infer a global phylogenetic tree of 3,399 language varieties. The inferred tree achieves a generalized quartet distance (GQD) to the Glottolog reference tree competitive with multiple baselines, while requiring only minutes of computation on a standard notebook GPU. Furthermore, the same representations capture diachronic concept stability: variance in pairwise distances across languages yields stability rankings that correlate significantly with established rankings. Ablation studies confirm that both the language-level objective and the use of phonetic feature vectors improved the inferred trees topology with regards to GQD. The framework thus provides a computationally efficient and fully automatic alternative for large-scale phylogenetic inference and offers a unified representation supporting downstream analyses at both the language and concept level.

</details>


### [143] [LexFlip: A Dissociation Diagnostic for Legal Meaning Preservation Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05296)
*Gaurab Baral*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出LexFlip数据集，通过微小改动反转法律条款效力但保留大部分词汇，发现现有语义评估指标无法有效检测此类变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前法律文本简化评估方法存在缺陷，仅依赖词汇重叠度无法准确衡量法律效力的变化，需要新的评估标准。

Method: 创建LexFlip数据集（373个魁北克法语法规的最小扰动样本），保持表面形式不变而反转法律效力，测试多种嵌入模型和BERTScore指标的表现。

Result: 现有语义指标对法律效力变化的敏感度极低（仅0.022-0.039），而双向NLI表现较好（0.670）；简单长度特征在FrJudge测试中优于所有语义指标。

Conclusion: 当前法律文本评估指标存在严重不足，需要开发能区分表面形式与法律效力的新评估方法。

Abstract: Does a simplified legal clause still say what the original said? The checks in current use cannot establish that it does: requiring an identical pair to score highest and an unrelated pair lowest moves lexical overlap and legal force together, so any monotone function of token overlap satisfies both. Our remedy is a dissociation, an item holding surface form fixed while legal force moves. We release LexFlip, 373 minimal perturbations of Quebec statutory French that reverse legal force while preserving 0.93 of the tokens, with a harness scoring metrics, regressors and prompted judges alike. The seven embedding and BERTScore metrics we test spend only 0.022 to 0.039 of their identical-to-unrelated range on such an edit, against 0.670 for bidirectional NLI, the one family the identical-pair check would disqualify. On FrJudge, against a measured human ceiling of r=0.597, a bare length feature outscores every semantic metric and has the lowest margin we measure.

</details>


### [144] [WearableQA: A Benchmark for Health Reasoning over Real-World Wearable Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05405)
*Ji Soo Lee,Xilun Chen,Pierce Chuang,Ashish Shenoy,Jason Wei,Dohwan Ko,Hyunwoo J. Kim,Benoit Corda*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了WearableQA，一个基于200名真实用户长达500天的可穿戴设备时间序列、生物标志物和人口统计数据构建的基准，包含4,084个10选项多选题，用于评估AI系统在真实用户纵向可穿戴记录上的推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准很少评估AI系统是否能对真实用户的纵向可穿戴记录进行推理，而可穿戴传感技术的进步使得持续监测生理和行为信号成为可能，因此需要一个能够反映真实世界可穿戴数据分布（包括设备噪声和个体间变异性）的评估基准。

Method: 采用双重 grounding 框架构建问题，结合文献支持的生理发现与统计验证的群体生理模式；设计16种问题类型，沿两个维度分类：数据推理与健康推理（区分纵向测量计算与生理生理解释），单信号与跨信号推理（区分单个信号推理与多信号整合）。

Result: 对14个专有和开源大语言模型的评估显示，WearableQA能有效区分模型能力，准确率范围从19.6%到72.9%（随机基线为10%），但大多数模型准确率低于60%，表明该基准尚未被解决。

Conclusion: WearableQA提供了一个现实且具有诊断性的基准，用于评估大语言模型在真实世界可穿戴数据上的推理能力，揭示了当前模型在此任务上的局限性，并为未来研究提供了方向。

Abstract: Recent advances in wearable sensing enable continuous monitoring of physiological and behavioral signals, yet existing benchmarks rarely evaluate whether AI systems can reason over a real user's longitudinal wearable record. We introduce WearableQA, a benchmark comprising 4,084 10-option multiple-choice questions constructed from the wearable time series, blood biomarkers, and demographics of 200 real users, each with up to 500 days of daily measurements. WearableQA preserves authentic wearable distributions that include device noise and inter-individual variability. To evaluate distinct reasoning capabilities, we introduce 16 question types organized along two complementary axes: data versus health reasoning, which distinguishes computation over longitudinal measurements from physiological interpretation; and single- versus cross-signal reasoning, which separates reasoning about individual signals from the integration of multiple signals. To construct reliable questions at scale, we adopt a dual-grounding framework that combines literature-grounded physiological findings with statistically validated population-grounded physiological patterns. This enables the capture of meaningful relationships observed in real-world wearable data. Evaluation of 14 proprietary and open-source LLMs demonstrates that WearableQA effectively differentiates model capabilities, with performance ranging from 19.6% to 72.9% against a 10% chance baseline. Moreover, WearableQA remains far from solved: most models achieve accuracies below 60%. Overall, WearableQA provides a realistic and diagnostic benchmark for evaluating LLM reasoning over real-world wearable data.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [145] [Deep Reinforcement Learning for Optimization of STAR-RIS Phase and Energy Splitting Coefficients in OTFS-NOMA Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04536)
*Rais. J. Gachaba,Manobendu Sarker,Anirban Bhowal*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合STAR-RIS、OTFS和NOMA技术的下行通信框架，并采用深度强化学习方法解决高移动性场景下的实时配置优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在STAR-RIS辅助的OTFS-NOMA通信框架中，延迟-多普勒移动性使得传统的交替优化方法在每个相干间隔内进行重新配置变得不切实际，需要一种能够实时映射信道状态到STAR-RIS配置的高效方法。

Method: 将STAR-RIS的相移和能量分割设计表述为具有闭式最大比传输波束成形和固定NOMA功率分配的约束非凸和速率最大化问题，并提出Beta-Space Soft Actor-Critic（SAC-BSE）最大熵深度强化学习代理，通过单次前向传播将观测到的信道实现映射到STAR-RIS配置。

Result: 仿真结果显示，在STAR-RIS每个分支上有两个NOMA复用用户的情况下，该方法快速收敛，在128倍用户速度范围内将和速率退化限制在约10%，并且在发射功率和STAR-RIS元件数量增加时，相比OTFS-only、NOMA-only、STAR-RIS-only、固定分割和模式切换基线方案获得一致增益。

Conclusion: 所提出的SAC-BSE深度强化学习方法能够有效解决高移动性场景下STAR-RIS的实时配置问题，在保持较低性能损失的同时显著提升系统性能，为STAR-RIS辅助的OTFS-NOMA系统提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper considers a downlink communication framework comprising a simultaneously transmitting and reflecting reconfigurable intelligent surface (STAR-RIS)-aided by orthogonal time frequency space (OTFS) and non-orthogonal multiple access (NOMA) technologies. Further, delay-Doppler mobility in such frameworks renders classical alternating optimization impractical for per-coherence interval reconfiguration. To mitigate such issues, the STAR-RIS phase-shift and energy-splitting design is formulated as a constrained, non-convex sum-rate maximization problem with closed-form maximum ratio transmission beamforming and fixed NOMA power allocation. To circumvent the per-interval re-optimization burden, a deep reinforcement learning (DRL) approach is adopted that maps observed channel realizations to STAR-RIS configurations through a single forward pass. Specifically, Beta-Space Soft Actor-Critic (SAC-BSE), a maximum entropy DRL agent, is proposed. Simulation results, with two NOMA-multiplexed users on each STAR-RIS branch, confirm rapid convergence, limit the sum-rate degradation to roughly 10\% across a 128-fold user-speed range, and yield consistent gains over OTFS-only, NOMA-only, STAR-RIS-only, fixed-split, and mode-switching baselines as transmit power and the number of STAR-RIS elements increase.

</details>


### [146] [Ultra-High Resolution Method for Multipath Within a Co-Delay-Doppler Bin in DFT-P-OCDM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04669)
*Mingxuan Han,Weile Zhang,Feifei Gao*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 提出TSUR框架，解决DFT-P-OCDM波形在双选择性信道中同延迟-多普勒仓内多路径分辨困难的问题，实现高分辨率感知与通信恢复的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在双选择性信道中，当多个物理路径落入同一共延迟-多普勒仓时，基于导频推断的可分辨延迟类数量少于实际物理路径数，导致路径数确定、分数多普勒偏移估计和信道重建困难。

Method: 推导DPF域中输入输出点关系，提出两阶段超高分辨率(TSUR)框架：第一阶段利用相位进展导频估计延迟，第二阶段利用泄漏样本估计多普勒和各延迟类内路径数；推导CRLB并分析导频配置对感知分辨率与通信恢复的权衡。

Result: 仿真表明TSUR能分辨共仓内同延迟路径，对延迟偏移鲁棒，且分数多普勒估计误差低于顺序提取和离网基线方法。

Conclusion: TSUR框架有效解决了同延迟-多普勒仓内多路径分辨问题，在感知分辨率与通信性能间取得良好平衡，优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: Communication systems can reuse their transmitted signals for sensing without dedicated radar transmissions. For an established DFT-preprocessed orthogonal chirp division multiplexing (DFT-P-OCDM) waveform, this task becomes difficult when several physical paths in a doubly selective channel fall into the same co-delay-Doppler bin. In this case, the number of resolvable delay classes inferred from the pilot may be smaller than the number of physical paths within the co-bin. This mismatch increases the difficulty of path number determination, fractional Doppler offset estimation, and channel reconstruction. We derive a pointwise relationship between the input and output in the DFT preprocessed Fresnel (DPF) domain for doubly selective channels with multiple paths within the co-delay-Doppler bin. Based on this input and output relation, we propose the two stage ultra high resolution (TSUR) framework. The first stage uses pilots of the phase progression to estimate delay, while the second stage uses the leakage samples to estimate the Doppler and the number of paths within each delay class. Furthermore, we derive CRLBs and analyze how the pilot configuration trades sensing resolution and communication recovery. Simulation results demonstrate that TSUR resolves same delay paths within a co-delay-Doppler bin, remains robust to exist delay offsets, and achieves lower fractional Doppler estimation errors than sequential extraction and off-grid baselines.

</details>


### [147] [The Cross-Correlation Distribution of the Niho-Type Decimation $d=4(2^m-1)+1$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04683)
*Maosheng Xiong,Haode Yan*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文解决了Niho型解数d=4(2^m-1)+1在有限域F_{2^{2m}}上的互相关分布问题，通过将问题转化为计算多项式f_a(x)在单位圆上的根数，利用特征和与Kloosterman和得到显式公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 互相关问题是序列设计中的经典问题，确定特定Niho型解数的互相关分布对于序列设计和编码理论具有重要意义。

Method: 将互相关问题转化为计算f_a(x)=x^7+ax^4+\bar a x^3+1在U_{q+1}上的根数分布问题，通过规范化四元素子集并利用预解式，将枚举问题转化为F_q上的方程，使用特征和、Kloosterman和及Carlitz定理进行计算。

Result: 获得了互相关分布中所有频率的显式公式，成功确定了该Niho型解数的完整互相关分布。

Conclusion: 该研究为有限域上特定Niho型解数的互相关分布提供了完整的理论解决方案，对序列设计领域有重要贡献。

Abstract: The cross-correlation problem is a classical problem in sequence design. In this paper, we determine the cross-correlation distribution of the Niho-type decimation $d=4(2^m-1)+1$ over $\mathbb F_{2^{2m}}$ for every positive integer $m$. With $q=2^m$, this is equivalent to determining the distribution of the number of roots in $U_{q+1}$ of $f_a(x)=x^7+ax^4+\bar a x^3+1$ for $a\in\mathbb F_{q^2}$, where $U_{q+1}=\{x\in\mathbb F_{q^2}:x^{q+1}=1\}$. The main difficulty is to count the four-element subsets of $U_{q+1}$ that may occur as root sets of $f_a$. We normalize such subsets by their product and study the resulting condition through the associated resolvent. This reduces the required enumeration to several equations over $\mathbb F_q$, which are evaluated by character sums and Kloosterman sums. The remaining mixed Kloosterman sum is related to a Kloosterman sum over $\mathbb F_{2^{2m}}$ and is evaluated using a theorem of Carlitz. Consequently, we obtain explicit formulas for all the frequencies in the cross-correlation distribution.

</details>


### [148] [Decoding Error Probability of the Random Code Ensemble over the Erasure Channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04688)
*Chin Hei Chan*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文推导了q元擦除信道下所有(n,M)_q码集在三种解码原则下的平均解码错误概率显式公式，并得出了相应的渐近错误指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在量化分析q元擦除信道中随机码集在不同解码策略（无歧义、列表、最大似然）下的平均性能表现，填补相关理论公式的空白。

Method: 通过概率论与编码理论推导，计算所有(n,M)_q码集的平均解码错误概率显式公式，并进一步针对码率为R的(n,q^{Rn})_q码集进行渐近分析。

Result: 获得了无歧义解码、列表解码及最大似然解码原则下的平均解码错误概率显式表达式，并推导出了对应的渐近错误指数。

Conclusion: 研究结果为评估q元擦除信道下随机编码方案的理论极限提供了精确的数学工具和性能基准。

Abstract: In this paper, we provide explicit formulas for the average decoding error probabilities of the ensemble of all $(n,M)_q$ codes over the erasure channel under unambiguous, list and maximum-likelihood decoding principles. Moreover, we derive the asymptotic error exponents for the ensemble of all $(n,q^{Rn})_q$ codes.

</details>


### [149] [Near-Field Physical-Layer Authentication Under Impersonation Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04879)
*Hajar El Hassani,Linda Senigagliesi,Arsenia Chorti*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究近场条件下的物理层认证，分析单天线和多天线攻击者的最优预编码策略，发现近场中距离差异可有效阻止欺骗攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统远场物理层认证仅依赖到达角，而近场中波束特征同时依赖角度和距离，需研究近场条件下欺骗攻击的可行性及防御机制。

Method: 通过最小化合法信号与攻击信号之间的均方误差，推导单天线攻击者的最优标量预编码器和多天线攻击者的最优预编码向量，基于二阶菲涅尔近似分析完美欺骗的条件。

Result: 单天线攻击者需与合法用户具有相同的角度和距离才能实现完美欺骗；多天线攻击者需合法用户波束向量位于其波束向量张成的子空间内；仿真表明近场中距离差异足以阻止相同到达角下的欺骗攻击。

Conclusion: 近场条件下距离信息可作为物理层认证的新维度，有效增强系统对欺骗攻击的防御能力。

Abstract: This paper studies physical-layer authentication (PLA) in the near-field regime under impersonation attacks. Unlike the far-field case, where the steering vector depends only on the angle of arrival (AoA), in the near field it depends on both angle and distance. We analyze the attack by minimizing the mean-square error (MSE) between the signal received from a legitimate transmitter Alice and the signal generated by an active attacker Eve. For a single-antenna Eve, we derive the optimal scalar precoder and show that, under the standard second-order Fresnel approximation, perfect impersonation is possible only if Eve has the same angle and the same distance from Bob as Alice. We then extend the analysis to a multi-antenna Eve and derive the optimal precoding vector. In this case, perfect impersonation is possible only if Alice steering vector belongs to the subspace spanned by Eve steering vectors. Simulation results show that, in the near field, a distance difference is sufficient to prevent a successful impersonation attack even when Alice and Eve have the same AoA, and confirm the analytical results.

</details>


### [150] [Minimum Schubert Codewords and Second-Minimum Grassmann Codewords](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04916)
*Muskan Khaneja,Prasant Singh*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文解决了Ghorpade和Singh在2018年提出的关于Schubert码$C_α(\ell, m)$最小权码字分类的猜想，并利用该分类证明了Grassmann码$C(\ell, m)$中第二小权码字的充要条件，最后给出了Grassmann码第二小权码字的计数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Ghorpade和Singh在2018年提出的关于Schubert码最小权码字分类的未解猜想，完善代数编码理论中关于Grassmann码和Schubert码的权分布理论。

Method: 通过分类Schubert码$C_α(\ell, m)$的最小权码字，利用该分类结果推导Grassmann码$C(\ell, m)$中第二小权码字的代数特征，并基于外代数结构进行枚举。

Result: 1. 完全解决了Ghorpade-Singh猜想，对所有$q$和$α$给出了Schubert码最小权码字的分类；2. 证明了Grassmann码第二小权码字的充要条件是其索引向量可分解为可分解$(m-\ell-2)$-向量与秩4的交错2-向量的乘积；3. 给出了Grassmann码第二小权码字的具体计数公式。

Conclusion: 该工作完善了Schubert码和Grassmann码的权分布理论，为代数几何码的结构分析提供了新的理论工具，特别是在第二小权码字的特征刻画和计数方面取得了突破性进展。

Abstract: In this paper, we give a classification of the minimum weight codewords of Schubert codes $C_α(\ell, m)$ by settling the conjecture proposed by Ghorpade and Singh in 2018, for all values of $q$ and all $α$. We use this classification to prove that a codeword of the Grassmann code $C(\ell, m)$ has the second minimum weight if and only if it is indexed by an element of $\bigwedge^{m-\ell}V$ that can be written as the product of a decomposable $(m-\ell-2)$-vector and an alternating $2$-vector of rank $4$. Finally, we give an enumeration of the second minimum weight codewords of the Grassmann code.

</details>


### [151] [Algebraic Geometry Codes Approach the Half-Singleton Bound with Constant Field Size](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05017)
*Neehar Verma,Camilla Hollanti,Razane Tajeddine*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分析评估码随机穿孔的通用框架，用于研究插入删除（insdel）错误下的线性码，证明了随机Reed-Solomon码和基于代数几何码的随机族在常数大小域上可逼近半Singleton界，并优化了对加性间隙$\varepsilon$的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的针对插入删除错误的线性码分析（如Con等人2025年的工作）往往依赖于对最长公共子序列的深入分析，论证复杂且对加性间隙$\varepsilon$的依赖较差（$2^{O(1/\varepsilon^2)}$）。作者旨在建立一个更通用、简洁的框架，仅通过评估域大小和底层函数空间非零函数的最大零点数来控制编辑距离，以简化证明并改进性能界限。

Method: 开发了一个针对评估码随机穿孔的通用理论框架。该框架将编辑距离的分析解耦，仅依赖于评估域的大小和函数空间中非零函数的最大零点数量。作者利用该核心定理，分别实例化了随机穿孔的Reed-Muller码、Reed-Solomon码以及源自渐近优良函数域塔的代数几何码。

Result: 1. 推广并简化了Con等人（ICALP 2025）的结果，避免了复杂的最长公共子序列分析。
2. 证明了随机Reed-Solomon码在线性大小域上逼近半Singleton界，并将对加性间隙$\varepsilon$的依赖从$2^{O(1/\varepsilon^2)}$显著改进为$2^{O(1/\varepsilon)}$。
3. 构造出了定义在常数大小域上的结构化线性码随机族，证明其同样能逼近半Singleton界。

Conclusion: 该通用框架不仅统一并简化了现有理论，还显著提升了随机线性码在插入删除信道下的性能界限，特别是证明了在常数大小域上存在逼近半Singleton界的结构化码族，为构造高效抗insdel错误的编码提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We study linear codes for insertion and deletion (insdel) errors through the lens of evaluation codes. We develop a general framework for analyzing random puncturings of evaluation codes, where the edit distance is controlled by only the size of the evaluation domain and the maximum number of zeros of a nonzero function in the underlying function space. Our proof generalizes the results of Con, Guo, Li, and Zhang (ICALP 2025), and simultaneously simplifies their arguments by avoiding an in-depth analysis of longest common subsequences. We demonstrate the applicability of our core theorem by instantiating it with random puncturings of Reed--Muller codes. We then recover the result that random Reed--Solomon codes approach the half-Singleton bound over linear-sized fields while also improving the dependence on the additive gap $\varepsilon$ from $2^{O(1/\varepsilon^2)}$ to $2^{O(1/\varepsilon)}$. Finally, by applying the framework to algebraic geometry codes arising from asymptotically good towers of function fields, we show that there exist randomized families of structured linear codes over constant-sized fields that approach the half-Singleton bound.

</details>


### [152] [Latency-Optimal Geo-Distributed Storage over Structured Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05229)
*Madhura Pathegama,Viveck Cadambe*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了地理分布式存储系统中的延迟最优文件分配问题，证明了对于固定文件数$k \geq 3$该问题为NP-hard，但针对加权树、特定加权环及高最小度单位权图等特定拓扑结构，提出了无需编码的结构化最优分配方案及高效构造算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在最小化地理分布式存储系统中检索原始文件的平均时间，解决在考虑通信延迟（建模为加权图边权）约束下的最优文件分配难题。

Method: 通过从支配集数问题（domatic number problem）归约证明问题的NP-hard性；同时分析特定网络拓扑（如加权树、特定加权环、单位权图），识别并证明存在无需编码的结构化最优分配（即每个节点及其$k-1$个最近邻节点存储不同的原始文件），并设计相应的高效构造算法。

Result: 确立了通用情况下文件分配问题的计算复杂性（NP-hard）；证明了加权树、特定加权环和具有足够大最小度的单位权图存在无需编码的最优分配结构；为这些图类提供了构建延迟最优文件分配的高效算法。

Conclusion: 虽然通用场景下的延迟最小化文件分配是计算困难的，但在多种自然且常见的网络拓扑结构中，存在简单的结构化最优解，可通过高效算法实现，无需依赖复杂的编码技术。

Abstract: We study latency-optimal file assignment in geo-distributed storage systems modeled as weighted graphs, where edge weights represent communication delays and each node stores one (possibly coded) file. Our goal is to minimize the average time required to retrieve an original file, taken uniformly over all nodes and files. We show that for every fixed number of files $k \geq 3$, computing a latency-minimizing assignment is NP-hard via a reduction from the domatic number problem. On the positive side, we identify natural network topologies that admit uncoded, structured optimal assignments in which, for every node, one can choose its $k$ closest nodes, including itself, so that they store distinct original files. We prove that every weighted tree, certain weighted cycles, and unit-weight graphs with sufficiently large minimum degree admit such assignments. For these graph classes, we provide efficient algorithms to construct latency-optimal file assignments.

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [153] [Scalable Neural Video Representation Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04273)
*Tianhao Peng,Ho Man Kwan,Fan Zhang,Shan Liu,David Bull*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出S-NVRC，一种基于隐式神经表示（INR）的可扩展视频编解码器，通过单一比特流实现细粒度的比特率和解码复杂度可扩展性，性能优于SHM 12.4和VTM-20.0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于INR的可扩展压缩方法通过增加网络层实现可扩展性，导致比特率与解码复杂度耦合，且性能无法与强可扩展/非可扩展编解码器相比。

Method: 采用特征网格的粗到细前缀和网络层的嵌套前缀，分别从单一编码（训练）中实现比特率和解码复杂度的可扩展性。

Result: 在UVG数据集上，BD-rate比SHM 12.4和多层VTM-20.0分别提升43.7%和5.6%，同时提供灵活的复杂度可扩展性。

Conclusion: S-NVRC通过单一嵌入比特流联合支持细粒度比特率和解码复杂度可扩展性，在保持灵活性的同时实现优越的编码效率。

Abstract: Scalable video coding (SVC) encodes a video into a layered bitstream consisting of a base layer and one or multiple enhancement layers, enabling decoding at different bitrate/quality/resolution operating points to accommodate diverse device capabilities and network conditions. Due to its practical flexibility, SVC has been incorporated into major video coding standards and has recently attracted growing interest for both scene-agnostic and scene-adaptive neural video codecs. Among the latter, Implicit neural representation (INR) based codecs achieve compression by overfitting a compact neural network to an individual video, offering fast decoding and competitive coding efficiency compared to scene-agnostic neural codecs. However, research on scalable INR-based compression remains in its infancy: these methods support scalable coding by introducing additional network layers, which couple the bitrate with the decoding complexity and also cannot achieve comparable performance with strong scalable/non-scalable codecs. In this context, this paper proposes S-NVRC, a scalable INR-based video codec that jointly supports fine-grained bitrate and decoding complexity scalability from a single embedded bitstream. It adopts a coarse-to-fine prefix for feature grids and a nested prefix for network layers, which scale bitrate and decoding complexity, respectively. The proposed S-NVRC spans a wide range of bitrate and decoding-complexity using a single encoding (training) and outperforms SHM 12.4 and the multi-layer VTM-20.0, by 43.7% and 5.6% in BD-rate on the UVG dataset, while also providing flexible complexity scalability. Implemented code will be provided.

</details>


### [154] [Multi-scale Image Representation Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04274)
*Tianhao Peng,Ho Man Kwan,Fan Zhang,Shan Liu,David Bull*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为MIRC的过拟合图像编解码器，通过端到端量化和熵编码优化率失真目标，并引入多尺度表示和跨阶段参数共享，在CLIC2020验证集上相比VVC实现了10.5%的BD-rate节省，同时提供可配置的解码复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的过拟合图像编解码器（如Cool-chic系列）虽然性能优越且解码复杂度低，但存在两个主要问题：1）网络权重在训练期间保持全精度，量化参数在训练后单独选择，未完全优化率失真目标；2）单尺度合成忽略了跨尺度冗余。

Method: 1. 提出MIRC编解码器，将所有编码组件（包括潜在变量、合成网络和熵模型）在单一率失真目标下进行量化和熵编码，采用NVRC的端到端压缩流程。2. 引入多尺度表示并结合跨阶段参数共享，以提高编码效率，同时仅增加少量传输开销。

Result: 在CLIC2020专业验证集上，MIRC相比VVC（VTM 22.0）实现了10.5%的BD-rate节省。此外，MIRC提供了一系列配置，解码复杂度范围在每像素1.2到2.9 kMAC之间，可根据部署目标选择解码预算。

Conclusion: MIRC通过端到端优化和多尺度表示有效解决了现有过拟合编解码器的不足，在保持低解码复杂度的同时显著提升了压缩性能，为不同部署场景提供了灵活的配置选择。

Abstract: Overfitted codecs have demonstrated promising performance for image and video compression. In particular, for image compression, the Cool-chic family of models has shown competitive performance against scene-agnostic models, with orders of magnitude lower decoding complexity at the cost of a longer overfitting process. However, these overfitted image codecs are not fully optimized toward the rate-distortion objective: their network weights remain in full precision during training, and the associated quantization parameters are selected in a separate post-training stage. Furthermore, their synthesis operates at a single scale, which overlooks cross-scale redundancy. In this paper, we propose MIRC, an overfitted image codec in which every coded component, including the latents, the synthesis network, and the entropy models, is quantized and entropy coded under a single rate-distortion objective, adopting the end-to-end compression pipeline of the neural video representation codec NVRC. We further introduce a multi-scale representation with cross-stage parameter sharing, which improves coding efficiency at a small transmitted overhead. On the CLIC2020 professional validation set, MIRC achieves a 10.5% BD-rate saving against VVC (VTM 22.0). Moreover, MIRC offers a family of configurations spanning 1.2 to 2.9 kMAC per pixel, so the decoding budget can be selected to match the deployment target.

</details>


### [155] [Cross-modal triage network: a multimodal deep learning framework for severity-based triage and visual explainability in chest radiographs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04357)
*Zinah Ghulam,Richa Mittal,Eranga Ukwatta*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出跨模态分诊网络（CMTN）用于胸部X光片严重度分诊和病理检测，虽在基准测试中表现优异，但与真实放射科医生判断存在显著差异，强调临床部署前需医生标注的真实数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胸部X光片数量增加导致分诊瓶颈，现有AI工具多为单模态二分类器，缺乏严重度感知，且多模态系统很少与放射科专家进行基准对比。

Method: 开发跨模态分诊网络（CMTN），融合Swin Transformer V2视觉编码器和PubMedBERT文本编码器，通过门控交叉注意力机制，使用MIMIC-CXR-JPG数据集训练，优化序数焦点损失和二元交叉熵损失。

Result: CMTN在基准测试中表现优异（QWK=0.9341，宏AUROC=0.9970），但临床审计显示与放射科医生判断一致性低（QWK=0.1399），54.3%热图达到临床可接受的空间定位。

Conclusion: CMTN展示了高效的多模态架构，但算法与医生判断的差异表明仅靠NLP衍生标签的基准性能不足，临床部署前需放射科医生标注的真实数据。

Abstract: Purpose: Increased number of chest radiograph (CXR) scans create a triage bottleneck, queueing urgent examinations behind routine ones. Existing AI tools are predominantly unimodal binary classifiers lacking severity awareness, and multimodal systems are rarely benchmarked against expert radiologists. To this end, we developed a multimodal deep learning framework for joint severity triage, pathology detection, and native visual explanation. Approach: We propose the cross-modal triage network (CMTN), fusing a Swin Transformer V2 visual encoder with a PubMedBERT text encoder via gated cross-attention. The CMTN was trained on 34,639 image-text pairs (12,489 patients) from MIMIC-CXR-JPG, optimizing an ordinal focal loss for four-tier severity triage and binary cross-entropy for 14 pathologies. Beyond quantitative benchmarking, attention heatmaps were evaluated against a blinded expert radiologist in a two-phase clinical audit comparing model triage output to expert severity assessment (100 cases) and grading spatial-semantic concordance (116 heatmaps). Results: The CMTN achieved strong ordinal agreement with reference labels (quadratic weighted kappa [QWK] = 0.9341, 95\% CI: 0.9219 to 0.9449) and macro-AUROC of 0.9970 across 14 pathologies, with 34~ms latency, outperforming the state-of-the-art BioViL multimodal baseline (QWK = 0.7679). However, the blinded Phase I clinical audit revealed substantially lower agreement with genuine radiologist judgment (QWK = 0.1399). Phase II found 54.3\% of heatmaps achieved clinically acceptable spatial localization. Conclusions: The CMTN demonstrated an efficient multimodal architecture for CXR triage. The divergence between algorithmic and radiologist agreement demonstrates that benchmark performance against NLP-derived labels is insufficient, highlighting the need for radiologist-labeled ground truth before clinical deployment.

</details>


### [156] [Ultrasound-Based Prediction of Cirrhosis Decompensation Using Large-Scale Computer Vision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04365)
*Guangyi Zhang,Peiyun Ni,Eugene Cheah,Rajat Chandra,Peng Guo,Raymond T. Chung,Anthony E. Samir*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于计算机视觉的新型成像方法，通过分析常规腹部超声图像来预测肝硬化失代偿的发生，为临床提供了一种无创、可扩展的风险分层工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肝硬化失代偿是疾病进程中的关键转折点，但临床医生缺乏可靠的无创工具来预测其发生。现有的实验室风险评分系统存在局限性，而超声检查具有普及性高、成本低、适合长期随访的优势，因此有必要开发基于超声图像的预测方法。

Method: 采用大规模计算机视觉模型分析常规腹部超声图像，提取传统实验室风险评分无法捕捉的预测特征。框架整合了自动化超声数据处理与现代深度学习架构，以识别失代偿高风险患者。

Result: 该框架能够在临床恶化发生前识别出失代偿高风险的肝硬化患者，提供了一种实用的无创策略，可作为现有临床评分系统的有效补充。

Conclusion: 基于深度学习的超声图像分析方法能够实现更早、更主动的代偿期肝硬化患者管理，具有 scalable risk stratification（可扩展风险分层）和长期随访的临床应用潜力。

Abstract: Decompensation represents a critical transition in the course of cirrhosis, yet clinicians have limited non-invasive tools to reliably predict its onset. In this study, we propose a novel imaging-based approach that leverages large-scale computer vision models to analyze routine abdominal ultrasound images and extract predictive features beyond those captured by traditional laboratory-based risk scores. Ultrasound is widely available, low cost, and suitable for longitudinal surveillance, making it an attractive modality for scalable risk stratification and long-term follow-up. Our framework integrates automated ultrasound data processing with modern deep learning architectures to identify patients at high risk of decompensation prior to the occurrence of clinical deterioration. This non-invasive strategy offers a practical complement to existing clinical scoring systems and may enable earlier, more proactive management of patients with compensated cirrhosis.

</details>


### [157] [Development and Evaluation of Ultrasound Image Learning Pipelines for MASLD Risk Stratification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04390)
*Guangyi Zhang,Xiaohong Wang,Eugene Cheah,Peng Guo,Brian A. Telfer,Theodore T. Pierce,Anthony E. Samir*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本研究开发了基于B模式和剪切波弹性成像（SWE）的深度学习超声图像分析流程，用于代谢功能障碍相关脂肪性肝病（MASLD）的纤维化分期和代谢功能障碍相关脂肪性肝炎（MASH）风险分层，发现SWE图像学习在纤维化分期中表现优于B模式图像学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代谢功能障碍相关脂肪性肝病（MASLD）影响约30%的普通人群，超声成像（包括B模式成像和剪切波弹性成像SWE）广泛用于无创纤维化评估，但基于深度学习的超声图像学习在MASLD风险分层中的作用尚未充分表征。

Method: 开发并评估使用B模式和SWE图像的超声图像学习流程，用于纤维化分期和识别有风险代谢功能障碍相关脂肪性肝炎（MASH）患者。纳入250次超声检查（每人一次），采用3折交叉验证，使用受试者工作特征曲线下面积（AUROC）评估模型性能。

Result: 端到端SWE图像学习在纤维化分期中达到与操作者引导SWE相当的性能。SWE图像学习在纤维化分期中一致优于B模式图像学习：F>=2（显著纤维化）AUROC从0.64提升至0.72（p=0.11），F>=3（晚期纤维化）从0.67提升至0.78（p=0.02），F4（肝硬化）从0.69提升至0.80（p=0.10）。

Conclusion: 研究结果突出了SWE图像学习在MASLD风险分层中的潜力，为无创评估代谢功能障碍相关脂肪性肝病提供了新的技术方向。

Abstract: Metabolic dysfunction-associated steatotic liver disease (MASLD) affects approximately 30% of the general population. Ultrasound-based imaging, including B-mode imaging and shear wave elastography (SWE), is widely used for noninvasive fibrosis assessment; however, the role of deep learning-based ultrasound image learning for MASLD risk stratification remains insufficiently characterized.
