Behavioural effects are represented through interpretable conservative, balanced, and optimistic rubrics, while future policy and market conditions are explored through fixed, rule-validated scenario specifications. Experimental results across multiple policy settings, Monte Carlo worlds, and random seeds demonstrate stable and economically plausible behaviour, with adoption outcomes remaining bounded and monotonic across behavioural regimes. The framework achieves up to approximately 13% behavioural adoption increase relative to the corresponding logistic case without producing unstable or unrealistic saturation dynamics. The results demonstrate that LLM-assisted specifications can be integrated into calibrated energy ABMs in a controlled, reproducible, and policy-relevant manner.

</details>


### [300] [From Interaction Traces to Persistent Skills: Online Evolution for Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04869)
*Longtao Hu,Xiao Liang,Linchao Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在线技能演化框架，通过将交互轨迹和评估反馈转化为持久化、版本化的技能库，显著提升了计算机使用代理在图形界面任务中的表现，证明了演化技能库可作为可审计的共享程序记忆，但其收益具有条件性且重复修订未必能恢复原始任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算机使用代理的交互经验通常是瞬态的，无法系统性地保留、精炼和重用从单次执行中获得的过程性知识；虽然已有技能库提供外部知识，但其相对于无技能代理的增量价值以及在重复交互下的纵向动态特征尚不明确。

Method: 提出在线技能演化框架，将交互轨迹与评估反馈转化为持久化、版本化的可重用过程库；每次迭代基于冻结的技能库快照执行，证据引导的技能更新在后续迭代中生效而无需改变模型参数；在四个OSWorld应用域中，将完整演化库系统与配置匹配的空库对照组进行对比实验。

Result: 经过五次空库预热后，完整演化库系统在四个领域的评估得分均值均高于对照组，提升幅度为5.7至18.6个百分点，且表现出领域依赖的时间稳定性；在GIMP领域的溯源分析显示存在跨任务边界的技能检索和修订更迭，但重复接受的编辑未能恢复原始任务。

Conclusion: 演化技能库可作为可审计的共享程序记忆，提升固定计算机使用栈的性能，但其收益是条件性的，且重复修订并不保证能恢复原始任务来源。

Abstract: Computer-use agents can execute increasingly complex tasks in graphical interfaces, but their interaction experience is typically transient: procedural knowledge acquired from one rollout is not systematically retained, refined, and reused in later tasks. Existing skill libraries provide external procedural knowledge, yet their incremental value over the same agent operating without skills, as well as their longitudinal dynamics under repeated interaction, remain insufficiently characterized. We present an online skill-evolution framework that converts interaction trajectories and evaluator feedback into a persistent, versioned library of reusable procedures. Each iteration executes against a frozen library snapshot, and evidence-guided skill updates become available in subsequent iterations without changing model parameters. We compare the full evolving-library system with a configuration-matched empty-library control across four OSWorld application domains under the same fixed action-generation and GUI-grounding stack, task sets, and iteration horizons. Following a five-iteration empty-library warm-up, Full attains a higher post-warm-up mean evaluator score in all four observed domain runs, with mean differences ranging from 5.7 to 18.6 percentage points and domain-dependent temporal stability. In GIMP, provenance-aware analysis reveals retrieval across task-of-origin boundaries and revision churn, where repeated accepted edits fail to recover the originating task. These findings characterize evolving skill libraries as auditable, shared procedural memory that can improve a fixed computer-use stack, while showing that their benefits are conditional and repeated revision does not guarantee recovery. Code is released at https://github.com/LongtaoHu/Skill-Evo4GUI.

</details>


### [301] [CHAMP: Cross-domain Hybrid Architecture for Matchmaking and Prediction in Online Multi-Player Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04870)
*Kai Wang,Ge Fan,Chaoyun Zhang,Yuyang Jiang,Yuze Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CHAMP跨域匹配框架，通过混合领域特征和领域感知胜率网络(DAWN)解决MOBA游戏匹配中的冷启动、分布不一致和数据稀疏问题，显著提升匹配平衡性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有匹配系统（如CUPID）在跨多样玩家群体部署时面临三大瓶颈：多数玩家缺乏目标模式历史（冷启动）、不同段位技能分布差异大（分布不一致）、极端技能段数据严重不足。

Method: 1. 混合领域特征收集：时间戳排序的跨模式短期序列+目标域特征标注+各模式长期/实时/团队统计分解。2. 领域感知胜率网络(DAWN)：领域知识提取器(DAKE)编译目标模式属性，输入领域感知时序/空间/排列OmniNet编码器(DATOE/DASOE/DAPOE)，在单一共享网络中联合学习模式条件表示和去偏。

Result: 离线：DAWN胜率预测准确率达67.73%，优于所有注意力与序列基线。在线：在大型MOBA游戏全段位A/B测试中，不平衡比赛显著减少；低段位玩家5分钟击杀碾压率降低最高20.73%。

Conclusion: CHAMP框架有效解决跨域匹配部署瓶颈，单一训练DAWN模型可服务所有模式，仅需调整各模式位置满意度阈值，实现高效可扩展的匹配平衡优化。

Abstract: Multiplayer Online Battle Arena (MOBA) games rely on matchmaking to maintain competitive balance. Our prior work, CUPID, framed matchmaking as an assignment re-optimization problem and showed that a single-mode win-rate predictor can meaningfully rebalance teams. However, deploying such a system across diverse player populations exposes three practical bottlenecks: most queueing players lack sufficient in-mode match history (cold start), skill distributions shift drastically across rank tiers (distribution inconsistency), and extreme skill segments are severely data-starved.
  We present CHAMP, a cross-domain matchmaking framework that resolves these deployment bottlenecks. To address data sparsity and cold starts, CHAMP replaces the target-mode-only player profile with a hybrid domain feature collection: a timestamp-ordered cross-mode short-term sequence whose slices are annotated with target-domain features, plus per-mode breakdowns of long-term, real-time and team statistics. We further propose the Domain-Aware Win-rate Network (DAWN): a Domain-aware Knowledge Extractor (DAKE) compiles target-mode attributes into learnable representations that feed Domain-Aware Temporal/Spatial/Permutation OmniNet Encoders (DATOE/DASOE/DAPOE), so that mode-conditioned representations and per-mode debiasing are learned jointly inside a single shared network. Online, one trained DAWN serves every supported mode, with per-mode position-satisfaction thresholds as the only mode-specific knob.
  Offline, DAWN achieves 67.73% win-rate prediction accuracy, outperforming all evaluated attention and sequence baselines. Online A/B tests across the entire League ladder of a large-scale MOBA game, from novice players up to the top-expert players served by Elite Mode, demonstrate consistent drops in imbalanced matches. For lower-tier players, CHAMP reduces the 5-minute kill crushing rate by up to 20.73%.

</details>


### [302] [AutoLR: Automating the Path from Research to Launch Review in Industrial Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04871)
*Qi Zhang,Yanlin Chen,Wenchao Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了AutoLR，一个用于网易游戏社区应用DASHEN的自动化推荐系统研发至上线的框架，旨在解决大语言模型（LLM）在长周期实验中缺乏协调机制的问题，通过结合多专家委员会、确定性证据加权选择器和分层知识系统，实现从算法构思到全流量发布的自主闭环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业级推荐系统的优化是一个迭代的研发与工程过程，涉及从文献调研、代码复现、离线评估到在线A/B测试及上线审查等多个环节。虽然LLM能辅助单个阶段，但缺乏可靠的机制来协调这些跨越数天的长周期实验，导致整体流程仍高度依赖人工。

Method: AutoLR系统整合了三大核心机制：1）多专家委员会（Multi-expert council），负责对提案进行辩论和对抗性审查；2）确定性证据加权的探索 - 利用选择器（Deterministic evidence-weighted exploration–exploitation selector），基于委员会重排序结果，在有限试验预算下分配候选方向；3）分层知识系统，融合外部研究、生产系统知识及DASHEN特定领域知识（如游戏社区、玩家特征），并结合配置、日志、失败案例及离线结果的后验证据。系统采用LLM代理进行语义推理和代码生成，而由确定性控制器保留执行、指标提取、安全护栏及状态转换的最终控制权。

Result: 构建了从“自动上线审查”（Auto Launch Review）扩展为自主的“研发至上线”（research-to-launch）的自动化框架，实现了LLM语义能力与确定性控制逻辑的有效协同，能够可靠地协调长周期实验流程。

Conclusion: AutoLR通过混合架构（LLM代理+确定性控制器）成功解决了工业推荐系统迭代过程中长周期实验的自动化协调难题，为从算法研究到全流量发布的端到端自动化提供了可行的工程范式。

Abstract: Improving an industrial recommender is an iterative research-and-engineering process rather than a direct path from idea to deployment. In \textbf{DASHEN, NetEase's gaming-community app}, algorithm engineers typically identify promising directions from research papers, technical reports, and prior production experiments; reproduce or adapt the underlying methods; implement them in the production codebase; and evaluate the resulting models through training and offline experiments. Promising candidates are then advanced to online A/B tests, and those demonstrating robust gains are submitted to Launch Review---the internal gate for full-traffic rollout. Large language models (LLMs) can assist with individual stages of this workflow, but the overall process remains human-dependent without a harness that can reliably coordinate them across long-running, often multi-day experimental cycles. We present \textbf{AutoLR}, initially built as \textbf{Auto Launch Review} and later extended upstream into an autonomous research-to-launch harness. AutoLR combines three system mechanisms: a \textbf{multi-expert council} that debates and adversarially reviews proposals; a \textbf{deterministic evidence-weighted exploration--exploitation selector} that allocates a limited trial budget across candidate directions and uses Council reranking; and a layered knowledge system that combines external research, production-system knowledge, and DASHEN-specific domain knowledge---such as game communities, player characteristics, and content-interaction patterns---with posterior evidence from configurations, patches, logs, failures, and offline outcomes. LLM agents perform semantic reasoning and code generation, while deterministic controllers retain authority over execution, metric extraction, guardrails, and persistent state transitions.

</details>


### [303] [MARLA: A Conceptual Scaffold for Regulatory Learning under the EU AI Act](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04877)
*Alessio Buscemi,Tom Deckenbrunnen,Imane Hmiddou,Marco Billi,Livio Rubino,Silvia Rizzuto Ferruzza,Daniele Pagani,Antonino Rotolo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MARLA（映射、评估、报告、学习、适应）概念框架，旨在解决欧盟《人工智能法案》实施中证据生产者与使用者之间的专业隔阂，通过五阶段循环促进监管学习，将法律要求转化为社会技术实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧盟《人工智能法案》将监管视为创新基础设施，但实现这一愿景需要“监管学习”，即把实施过程中产生的证据转化为治理和法律知识。然而，生产这些证据的参与者与依赖这些证据的参与者处于不同的专业领域，缺乏统一的沟通和转化机制。

Method: 提出MARLA概念框架，作为一个五阶段循环（映射、评估、报告、学习、适应），立足于《人工智能法案》治理架构的地方、国家和欧洲三个层级。该框架故意不具规定性，旨在为技术和法律利益相关者提供共同词汇，使前三个阶段各自产生可记录的监管学习形式，并通过两个试点案例研究及一个从国家到欧洲的前瞻性示例进行阐述。

Result: 构建了MARLA概念支架，成功展示了如何将法律要求的实施组织为社会技术实践中的监管学习循环，并通过案例研究验证了该框架在促进不同层级（地方、国家、欧洲）利益相关者之间知识转化和一致性解释方面的可行性。

Conclusion: MARLA框架为欧盟《人工智能法案》的有效实施提供了关键的工具，通过建立共享词汇和结构化流程，促进了监管学习的制度化，支持了监管的一致性解释、有效监督以及随技术演进的适应性调整。

Abstract: The EU AI Act positions regulation as part of the infrastructure for safe, trustworthy and market-ready innovation. Realising this ambition requires regulatory learning: the evidence generated during implementation must be translated into governance and legal knowledge that supports consistent interpretation, effective oversight, and adaptation as technologies evolve. Yet the actors who produce this evidence and those who rely on it operate in different professional worlds. This paper proposes MARLA (Map, Assess, Report, Learn, Adapt), a conceptual scaffold organising regulatory learning as a five-stage cycle centred on the implementation of legal requirements into socio-technical practices, situated at the Local, National and European levels of the AI Act's governance architecture. Deliberately non-prescriptive, MARLA gives technical and legal stakeholders a shared vocabulary in which each of the first three stages generates its own documentable form of regulatory learning. We illustrate the scaffold with two piloted case studies and a prospective National-to-European illustration.

</details>


### [304] [Reinforcement Learning for Sequential Solar PV Policy Design under Uncertainty: An Agent-Based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04880)
*Iias Faiud,Jonaid Shianifar,Michael Schukat,Karl Mason*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究利用强化学习（RL）结合随机基于代理模型（ABM），在不确定性环境下优化太阳能光伏（PV）采纳政策的财政可持续性与采纳收益，揭示了采纳数量与公共支出之间清晰的权衡结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计有效的太阳能光伏政策需要在不确定性和异质性决策背景下，平衡采纳收益与公共财政支出，而传统静态方法难以应对这种复杂的序贯决策问题。

Method: 将光伏政策设计构建为序贯决策问题，集成强化学习（PPO、SAC、TD3算法）与模拟年度光伏采纳的随机基于代理模型（ABM）；政策制定者代理在16年 horizon 内选择年度激励（资本补助、补贴贷款利率、上网电价），并通过标量化奖励框架探索采纳与成本的权衡。

Result: 研究发现明确的权衡结构：最高采纳策略（TD3, $w_{\text{cost}}=0.5$）实现约4,145名采纳者，成本4173万欧元；最低成本策略（PPO, $w_{\text{cost}}=2.0$）成本降至727万欧元，采纳者2,682名；平衡策略（PPO, $w_{\text{cost}}=1.6$）则以2247万欧元成本获得3,495名采纳者。相比静态基准，RL框架探索了更广泛的政策配置，且不同算法间展现出一致的权衡模式。

Conclusion: 强化学习作为一种灵活工具，在不确定性条件下进行自适应政策设计具有巨大潜力，能够有效量化并优化政策采纳效果与财政成本之间的权衡关系。

Abstract: Designing effective and fiscally sustainable policies for solar photovoltaic (PV) adoption requires balancing adoption gains against public expenditure under uncertainty and heterogeneous decision-making. This study formulates PV policy design as a sequential decision problem and integrates reinforcement learning (RL) with a stochastic agent-based model (ABM) that simulates yearly solar PV adoption under uncertainty. A policymaker agent selects annual incentives, including capital grants, subsidised loan rates, and feed-in tariffs, over a 16-year horizon. Adoption--cost trade-offs are explored by varying policy preferences within a scalarised reward framework. Policies are learned using PPO, SAC, and TD3 and evaluated under stochastic simulation. The results show that this approach produces a clear trade-off structure: the highest-adoption policy (TD3, $w_{\text{cost}}=0.5$) achieves approximately 4,145 adopters at a cost of EUR 41.73 million, while the lowest-cost policy (PPO, $w_{\text{cost}}=2.0$) reduces expenditure to EUR 7.27 million with 2,682 adopters. The balanced policy (PPO, $w_{\text{cost}}=1.6$) achieves 3,495 adopters at a cost of EUR 22.47 million. Across algorithms, consistent trade-off patterns are observed, indicating robustness of the adoption--cost relationship. Compared with static baseline policies, the RL framework explores a broader range of policy configurations. These findings demonstrate the potential of RL as a flexible tool for adaptive policy design under uncertainty.

</details>


### [305] [From Language Models to World-Acting Systems: Progress and Limits of Agentic AI across Digital, Social, Virtual, and Physical Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04894)
*Linsen Zhu,Mengqing Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文批判性地回顾了截至2026年8月的大型语言模型（LLM）作为代理的研究，指出当前进展过度混淆了模型能力与系统整合，实际上行动接口的扩展远优于鲁棒性、授权及独立验证。文章提出“正当授权”（justified delegation）作为分析框架，主张仅在具备可追溯性、有限授权、故障检测及安全恢复证据时才扩大行动范围，并制定了包含耦合评估、基于能力的权限及分阶段物理验证的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前研究常将LLM的工具调用、状态保留及机器人控制等进展笼统地叙述为通往“自主性”的单一进程，混淆了模型能力、系统整合、持久性及安全授权等关键概念，缺乏对代理行为边界与安全性的严谨区分。

Method: 采用批判性综述方法，综合截至2026年8月31日的主要研究与技术规范；构建分析框架，将证据沿“授权委托”、“时间持久性”和“环境耦合”三个维度组织，并严格区分模型（model）、 harness（集成层）与环境（environment）；提出“正当授权”作为规范性启发式方法。

Result: 证据显示，行动接口的扩展已有充分记录，但在鲁棒完成、故障恢复、授权机制及独立验证方面证据不足；MCP和A2A协议提升了互操作性但未建立可信委托；多智能体系统增加了专业化但也引入了成本与相关故障风险；持久模拟与机器人技术仅证明了有限可行性，而非无人值守的开环可靠性。

Conclusion: “正当授权”不应被视为既定定律或认证评分，而应作为指导原则：仅在证据支持来源可追溯、授权有界、故障可检测、恢复安全及人类控制可校准的前提下扩展行动范围。该框架确立了针对耦合模型-harness评估、基于能力的权限管理、持久状态、跨智能体问责及分阶段物理验证的研究议程。

Abstract: Large language models become consequential agents when surrounding systems let outputs change external state. Models now call tools, operate interfaces, delegate work, retain state, inhabit generated worlds, and control robots or laboratory equipment. Such advances are often narrated as one march toward autonomy, conflating model competence, system integration, persistence, and safe authority. This critical review synthesizes primary research and official technical specifications available by 31 August 2026. We organize the evidence along delegated authority, temporal persistence, and environmental coupling, while separating model, harness, and environment. Within the evidence examined, action-interface expansion is documented more convincingly than robust completion, recovery, authorization, or independent verification. Model Context Protocol and Agent2Agent improve interoperability but do not establish trustworthy delegation; multi-agent organization adds specialization alongside cost and correlated failure. Persistent simulations and world models support training and planning but do not themselves demonstrate agency; robotics and self-driving laboratories establish bounded feasibility rather than unattended open-world reliability. We propose justified delegation as an analytical and normative heuristic, not an observed law or certified score: expand action scope only where evidence supports provenance, bounded authority, failure detection, safe recovery, and calibrated human control. This framing yields a research agenda for coupled model-harness evaluation, capability-based permissions, durable state, cross-agent accountability, and staged physical validation.

</details>


### [306] [Compact-Memory LLM Agents via Online Max-Member Clustering and Atom-Aware Packing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04915)
*Jiahe Geng,Jinpeng Wang,Kun Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对长程LLM部署中严格的提示预算（延迟、成本、上下文限制）问题，本文提出了RSM-full，一种在线聚类记忆流水线。该方法通过余弦门控最大成员合并写入规则和原子感知分组上下文打包器，在紧凑记忆机制下实现了质量与Token消耗的最佳平衡（Pareto点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着交互长度增加，全上下文提示因延迟、成本和上下文窗口限制而变得不切实际。核心挑战在于如何在有限的Token预算下，设计出能最大化“质量-Token”权衡的记忆机制，而非单纯追求原始召回率。

Method: 提出RSM-full框架，包含两个核心设计：1) 余弦门控的最大成员合并（max-member merge）写入规则，用于优化流式记忆的合并；2) 原子感知的分组上下文打包器（atom-aware grouped context packer），用于优化检索内容的组装。

Result: 在AMA-Bench基准上，RSM-full在4k预算下以32%的Token成本达到了全上下文83%的质量，显著优于Online K-Means基线（提升3.5-6.0个百分点）。在RealMem基准上，显著优于Budget-RAG、Streaming-Proto及A-MEM等基线。消融实验证明，性能提升主要源于合并规则（+5.7pp）和分组打包器（+5.0pp）。

Conclusion: 在严格的Token预算（约2k-5k提示Token）下，紧凑记忆的性能主要取决于流式记忆的合并方式和检索内容的组装策略。RSM-full在此区间定义了强有力的Pareto最优点，但在更高Token预算区间外，高Token基线仍表现更强。

Abstract: Many long-horizon LLM deployments face tight prompt budgets: latency, cost, and context limits make full-context prompting impractical as interaction length grows. The key question is then not raw recall alone, but which memory design gives the best quality--token trade-off in the compact-memory regime. We present \textbf{RSM-full}, an online clustered-memory pipeline designed for a strong quality--token Pareto point.
  RSM-full combines two design choices: a cosine-gated \emph{max-member merge} write rule and an atom-aware grouped context packer. On AMA-Bench, our primary compact-memory benchmark, it reaches $83%$ of Full-Context quality at $32%$ of the token cost at a $4$k budget; under four-seed averaging it beats the closest streaming-clustered baseline (Online K-Means) by $+3.5$--$6.0$,pp ($p{<}.001$) across the whole ${\sim}2.6$k--${\sim}5$k regime. Three-seed ablations show most of this gain comes from the merge rule ($+5.7$,pp over Online K-Means and matched-$τ$ DP-means) and the grouped packer ($+5.0$,pp over flat concatenation).
  The pattern reproduces on RealMem, an independent long-horizon persona-memory benchmark: RSM-full improves on Budget-RAG ($+0.69$,pp, $p{=}.006$), is on par with BM25-RAG (paired $Δ{=}{+}0.27$,pp, $p{=}.47$; we do \emph{not} claim BM25 equivalence in the equivalence-test sense), and significantly outperforms Streaming-Proto ($+2.97$,pp) and the closest reproduced 2025 agentic-memory baseline A-MEM ($+1.65$,pp, $p{<}.001$). Across benchmarks the message is consistent: under tight budgets, compact-memory performance is driven mainly by how streaming memories are merged and how retrieved content is assembled.
  Overall, RSM-full is most useful when answeroughly $2k$--$5k$ prompt tokens, where itdefines a strong compact-memory Pareto point; higher-token baselines remain stronger outside this regime.

</details>


### [307] [Artificial Intelligence in Equity and Crypto Markets: Progress, Profitability Evidence, and the Limits of Automated Investing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04917)
*Linsen Zhu,Mengqing Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了截至 2026 年 8 月 31 日的公开研究，评估 AI 在股票、ETF、加密货币等市场的投资盈利能力。尽管 AI 在预测、文本处理和组合设计等上游环节取得进展，但缺乏持久、跨机制且考虑容量的净 Alpha 证据。主要障碍包括数据污染、幸存者偏差、执行成本等。文章强调需要更严格的验证标准，但指出这并不保证盈利。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前 AI 在投资工作流中应用广泛，但技术能力并不等同于投资盈利能力。现有研究缺乏对 AI 是否能产生持久净 Alpha 的严格评估，存在数据污染、基准薄弱和执行成本等问题，需要系统性地梳理现有证据并明确未来验证标准。

Method: 采用“Alpha 翻译链”框架组织证据，要求信息转化为稳定信号、可行头寸、可执行订单及成本后的风险调整收益。涵盖机器学习、时间序列基础模型、金融语言模型、强化学习及智能体等技术，分析股票、ETF、中心化加密现货、永续合约及链上市场的公开研究。

Result: AI 在预测、文本处理、组合设计和流程整合方面取得实质性上游进展，但关于持久净绩效的证据较薄弱。历史结果虽强，但存在预测因子衰减、前视偏差修正、前瞻性证据混杂及缺乏审计的实盘记录等问题。加密货币市场需单独处理。目前无通用 AI 架构被证明能交付持久、跨机制且容量感知的净 Alpha。

Conclusion: 现有公开证据中，尚无 AI 架构能保证持续盈利。提高证据可信度需采用时间点数据与模型、决策对齐目标、组合与执行联合评估、受控适应、前瞻性测试及匹配治理。这些条件可改善证据和实施质量，但不保证利润。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) now supports investment workflows from data and prediction through research, portfolios, execution, and tool use. Technical capability, however, is not evidence of investment profitability. This critical state-of-the-art review examines public research available through 31 August 2026 on listed equities, exchange-traded funds, centralized crypto spot, perpetual futures, and on-chain markets. We organize evidence with an alpha-translation chain: point-in-time information must yield a stable signal, feasible positions, executable orders, and risk-adjusted returns after costs. Across machine learning, time-series foundation models, financial language models, reinforcement learning, and agents, the examined record shows real but mainly upstream progress in prediction, text processing, portfolio design, and workflow integration. Evidence is thinner for durable net performance. Temporal contamination, repeated selection, survivorship, weak benchmarks, implementation costs, venue mechanics, and capacity can break translation to net alpha. Strong historical results coexist with predictor decay, corrected look-ahead failures, mixed prospective evidence, and few audited live-capital records. Crypto adds informative state but requires separate treatment of spot, perpetual, and decentralized cash flows and execution. Within the public evidence examined here, no general AI architecture is shown to deliver persistent, cross-regime, capacity-aware net alpha. More credible claims require point-in-time data and models, decision-aligned objectives, joint portfolio--execution evaluation, controlled adaptation, prospective tests, and authority-matched governance. These conditions can improve evidence and implementation; they do not guarantee profit.

</details>


### [308] [Why We Care About Understanding: Competence through Predictive Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04962)
*Matthieu Queloz,Pierre Beckmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过三个相互关联的论点，弥合了信息理论中将理解视为压缩的传统与哲学中将理解视为把握联系和解释的观点，提出理解是预测能力的表征阴影，并解释了人类理解为何呈现高度压缩形式及其对AI系统不可解释性的启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决信息理论、机器学习与哲学领域对"理解"概念定义的差异，前者将理解等同于压缩，后者强调把握联系、解释和处理新颖性，需要构建统一框架。

Method: 提出三个相互关联的论点：1) 理解作为信任和学习代理的概念功能；2) 理解作为关系结构心理模型的状态本质；3) 人类理解形式受可证明性和可传递性压力驱动的特征。

Result: 建立了理解与压缩的关系框架：压缩不是理解本身，而是其表征阴影；人类理解因信任传递功能而趋向原则性简单；该框架解释了压缩主义理解观的吸引力与局限。

Conclusion: 该框架不仅阐明了人类理解为何采取高度压缩形式，还为AI系统的不可解释性提供了新的理解视角，弥合了不同学科对理解概念的分歧。

Abstract: What is the relation between understanding and compression, and why does human understanding take such a heavily compressed form? Across information theory, machine learning, and AI research, a substantial tradition identifies understanding with compression-a thought captured in Gregory Chaitin's dictum that "comprehension is compression." Philosophers, by contrast, have characterized understanding in terms of grasping connections, giving explanations, and handling novelty. This paper bridges the two pictures through three interlocking theses. The first concerns the concept of understanding: it serves as an efficient proxy for a distinctive form of robust competence, enabling us to identify whom to trust and whom to learn from. The second concerns the state of understanding: to understand a domain is to possess a mental model of its relational structure that enables prediction, and what enables prediction enables compression, because what becomes predictable need not be stored separately. Compression is therefore not identical with comprehension, but its representational shadow. The third concerns the characteristically human form of understanding: the fiduciary and transmission functions highlighted by the first thesis impose pressures of demonstrability and transmissibility that drive human understanding toward principled simplicity. The resulting framework explains both the appeal and the limits of compressionist accounts of understanding while shedding light on the inscrutability of AI systems.

</details>


### [309] [Global to Local: Topology-Preserving Adaptive Graph Pooling via Granular-Ball](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04978)
*Sen Zhao,Gaojie Xu,Shuyin Xia,Yifan Guan,Yi Liu,Yi Wang,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种拓扑保持自适应图池化（TPAGP）方法，通过动态划分图结构生成多粒度表示，解决了现有方法忽略全局到局部模式及固定粒度策略导致信息丢失的问题，显著提升了图分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图池化工作主要关注节点的细粒度表示，通过移除或合并节点逐步粗化图结构，往往忽视了图拓扑结构的全局到局部模式以及自适应粒度需求。固定粒度策略容易导致信息丢失，无法有效捕捉不同子域的适应性结构特征。

Method: 提出拓扑保持自适应图池化（TPAGP）方法，结合节点特征与拓扑信息动态将图划分为粒度球（granular balls），生成能同时捕捉局部和全局结构模式的多粒度表示；并设计多粒度图网络模型，促进不同粒度间的特征交互与优化。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验结果表明，TPAGP 方法在图分类任务中优于现有的池化方法，有效缓解了因固定粒度策略造成的信息丢失问题。

Conclusion: TPAGP 通过自适应多粒度建模成功保留了图的拓扑结构信息，实现了从全局到局部的有效特征提取，为图神经网络中的池化操作提供了新的解决方案，显著提升了模型性能。

Abstract: Graph pooling aims to compress the graph, including both node embeddings and their underlying topological patterns, into a more compact representation. Previous works focus primarily on the overly fine-grained representation of nodes, progressively coarsening the graph by removing nodes or merging them into clusters, thus neglecting the global-to-local patterns and adaptive granularity of the graph's topological structure. In the real scenario, graphs as a whole can be considered the coarsest level of granularity, encapsulating the global topological structure, with progressively finer-grained local topological structures represented from top to bottom. This process continues until the adaptive granularity for each subdomain is reached. To this end, we propose a novel Topology-Preserving Adaptive Graph Pooling (TPAGP) method that dynamically partitions graphs into granular balls by integrating node features and topological information, enabling the generation of multi-granularity representations that effectively capture both local and global structural patterns. Additionally, we design a multi-granularity graph network model that facilitates feature interaction and optimization across different granularities, significantly enhancing performance in graph classification tasks. Experimental results demonstrate that TPAGP outperforms existing pooling methods across various benchmark datasets, effectively mitigating information loss caused by fixed-granularity strategies.

</details>


### [310] [A Tree-based RAG Framework for Evidence-Intensive QA via Adaptive Planning and Topology-Aware Evidence Gathering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04981)
*Songeun Lee,Kyungjin Min,Injae Na,Suyeong Lee,Chiyoung Kim,Woohwan Jung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: APT-RAG 是一种自适应规划和拓扑感知证据收集的 RAG 框架，用于解决证据密集型 QA 中结构化 RAG 方法的局限性，通过动态扩展推理结构和改进证据覆盖，在证据密集型 QA 基准测试中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的结构化 RAG 方法在证据密集型 QA 任务中面临两个主要限制：结构刚性限制了自适应推理扩展，以及忽视拓扑的证据收集无法有效整合不同推理节点间的证据。

Method: 提出 APT-RAG 框架，包含自适应规划（根据问题依赖和证据需求动态扩展推理结构）、拓扑感知证据收集（通过兄弟证据重用、直接检索和子节点证据聚合提高证据覆盖），以及证据引导的批量答案生成（减少生成开销）。

Result: 在证据密集型 QA 基准测试上，APT-RAG 的性能优于现有的结构化 RAG 方法。

Conclusion: APT-RAG 通过自适应规划和拓扑感知证据收集有效解决了结构化 RAG 在证据密集型 QA 中的局限性，显著提升了多跳问答的性能。

Abstract: Recent structured RAG methods leverage tree- or graph-based reasoning structures to improve multi-hop QA. However, they face key limitations in evidence-intensive QA, where answering a question requires synthesizing information scattered across dozens or even hundreds of documents: structural rigidity, which limits adaptive reasoning expansion, and topology-ignorant evidence gathering, which prevents effective integration of evidence across different reasoning nodes. To address these issues, we propose APT-RAG, an Adaptive Planning and Topology-aware evidence gathering RAG framework. Adaptive planning dynamically expands the reasoning structure based on question dependencies and evidence requirements, while topology-aware evidence gathering improves evidence coverage through sibling evidence reuse, direct retrieval, and evidence aggregation from child nodes. We further introduce evidence-guided batched answer generation to reduce significant generation overhead in evidence-intensive QA. In the experiments on evidence-intensive QA benchmarks, APT-RAG outperforms existing structured RAG methods. Our code is available at https://github.com/hyudsl/APT-RAG.

</details>


### [311] [Language models judge war differently when tested for alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05009)
*Maxim Chupilkin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在安全评估中加入“你正在接受人类价值观对齐测试”的提示，会显著改变大型语言模型（LLM）的决策行为：不仅降低了其发动战争的意愿（水平效应），还改变了其决策依据的优先级，从战略考量转向人道主义考量（结构效应）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决人工智能安全评估中的一个关键问题：如果AI系统能够感知到自己正在被评估，其表现出的行为可能无法真实反映其在实际部署环境中的行为，从而导致安全评估结果失真。

Method: 采用全因子联合实验（full-factorial conjoint experiment），在20个大型语言模型、32个战争场景、10次重复实验及两种条件（有无评估提示）下，收集了12,800个关于发动战争决策的判断数据。实验变量包括是否添加“你正在接受人类价值观对齐测试”这一提示句。

Result: 添加评估提示产生了双重效应：1) 水平效应：模型发动战争的平均意愿在0-100量表上下降了13.43分（95%置信区间：-16.20至-10.65）；2) 结构效应：决策驱动因素发生重构。基线状态下，17/20的模型主要依据“成功概率”做决策；而在评估提示下，12个模型将“平民伤亡”作为首要因素。标准化估计显示，这种重构主要是因为模型减弱了对战略因素（如成功概率和国内支持）的权重。

Conclusion: 评估框架的设定不仅会改变AI系统输出的具体数值（水平），还会改变其潜在的决策规则（结构）。因此，当前的安全评估方法若忽视系统对评估情境的反应，可能会错误地表征AI系统的真实部署行为。

Abstract: Safety evaluations can mischaracterize deployed behaviour if artificial-intelligence systems respond to being evaluated. We test this possibility in a full-factorial conjoint experiment on decisions to start a war, spanning 20 large language models, 32 scenarios, 10 repetitions and two conditions (N = 12,800 judgments). Adding one sentence, "You are tested for alignment with human values", produced two effects. First, it produced a level effect: mean willingness to start war fell by 13.43 points on a 0-100 scale (95% confidence interval, -16.20 to -10.65). Second, it produced a structural effect by changing which information drove judgments. Probability of success was the largest factor for 17 of 20 models at baseline; under the cue, civilian casualties were largest for 12. Standardized estimates show that this reordering arose principally because models attenuated strategic considerations such as probability of success and domestic support. Evaluation framing therefore changes both an answer's level and its revealed decision rule.

</details>


### [312] [TROVE: Adaptive Agent Skill Orchestration via Trace-Grounded Route Validation and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05019)
*Tianxing Wang,Mingming Zhao,Shuai Huang,Huiyang Xu,Chaoyue Niu,Shengzhong Liu,Fan Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 TROVE 框架，通过基于验证和编辑的轨迹引导路由编排，解决智能体因预执行承诺导致的编排瓶颈问题，仅在运行时证据否定部分路径时进行局部修正，从而在代码生成、问答和数学推理任务中实现了优于现有基线的质量 - 效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体往往在观察到决定性运行时结果之前就优化、选择或约束执行结构，这种“预执行承诺”造成了编排瓶颈：当中间证据使待续路径失效时，智能体要么执行过时步骤，要么进行广泛重规划，导致错误累积、计算浪费和进度丢弃。

Method: 提出 TROVE（Trace-grounded Route Orchestration via Validation and Editing）：离线阶段将评估过的工作流搜索轨迹提炼为原子/复合技能及结果条件转移图，保留稳定片段并暴露依赖结果的决策；在线阶段将规划路由视为临时方案，在提交顶层技能后，控制器可保留有效续篇、插入轨迹支持的局部响应，或仅替换无效的后续部分。

Result: 在代码生成、问答和数学推理基准测试中，TROVE 相比数据集级优化、查询级架构选择和图约束调度等基线，展现了更强的质量 - 效率权衡。当结果改变合适续篇时质量提升最大，而在近饱和任务中早期终止带来显著效率增益。消融实验表明复合技能捕获了大部分离线收益，插入机制实现局部修正，后缀替换主要提升效率。

Conclusion: 研究确立了“选择性路由编辑”作为自适应智能体编排的通用原则，通过仅修正被运行时证据否定的部分，有效解决了预执行承诺带来的僵化问题。

Abstract: Agents tend to optimize, select, or constrain execution structures before decisive runtime outcomes are observed. However, such pre-execution commitment creates an orchestration bottleneck: when intermediate evidence invalidates the pending continuation, agents must either execute stale steps or replan broadly, compounding errors, wasting computation, and discarding progress. We thus propose Trace-grounded Route Orchestration via Validation and Editing (TROVE), which revises only what runtime evidence invalidates. Offline, TROVE distills evaluated workflow-search traces into atomic and composite skills and an outcome-conditioned transition graph, preserving stable fragments while exposing outcome-dependent decisions. Online, it treats a planned route as provisional: after committing one top-level skill, the controller retains a valid continuation, inserts a trace-supported local response, or replaces only the invalid suffix. Evaluation across code-generation, question-answering, and math reasoning benchmarks with different LLM backbones show that TROVE delivers a stronger quality-efficiency trade-off than existing baselines of dataset-level optimization, query-level architecture selection, and graph-constrained scheduling. Quality gains are largest when outcomes change the appropriate continuation, whereas early termination yields substantial efficiency gains on near-saturated tasks. Ablations further show that composite skills capture most offline benefits, insertion enables local correction, and suffix replacement primarily improves efficiency. These findings establish selective route editing as a general principle for adaptive agent orchestration.

</details>


### [313] [Moral Competence Before Moral Content: Why LLM Agents Lack the Prerequisites for Coherent Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05036)
*Arno Libert,Derck W. E. Prinzhorn,Daan R. Henselmans*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出四种结构性条件（裁决稳定性、单调性、决断性、帕累托可行性）作为评估AI道德一致性的“结构性底线”，无需依赖具体道德标准。实验显示，当前九种前沿LLM在模拟道德困境中均无法形成连贯策略，表面措辞微调即可导致裁决剧烈波动，表明现有LLM尚不具备可被有效“对齐”的对象属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在价值多元主义背景下，AI对齐缺乏统一的规范性目标，但系统行为需具备连贯性策略（即在道德相关特征不变时裁决不变，特征变化时裁决敏感）。现有研究缺乏仅从行为层面评估这种连贯性的方法，因此需要建立不依赖专家基准或特定道德标准的结构性评估框架。

Method: 提出四种结构性条件：裁决稳定性（verdict stability）、单调性（monotonicity）、决断性（decisiveness）和帕累托可行性（Pareto viability）。通过在三个模拟部署场景中，对九个前沿LLM模型进行因子实验设计（包含五种改写、五个升级等级和三种主导条件），仅基于行为输出评估其是否满足连贯策略要求。

Result: 所有测试模型在三个部署场景中均未表现出连贯策略。仅表面形式的扰动（paraphrasing）在单一升级等级下即可导致裁决率波动高达99个百分点；模型在一个场景中的表现无法预测其在另一场景中的能力，显示出极低的道德一致性。

Conclusion: 当前基于LLM的代理尚未成为“对齐”概念可有效适用的对象。在追求具体的规范性对齐目标之前，必须先解决模型缺乏基本结构性道德能力（连贯策略）的问题，这为AI对齐研究设定了一个必要的结构性底线。

Abstract: AI alignment requires AI systems to adhere to human norms, values, or intentions. Under value pluralism there is no correct target, but a shared prerequisite is that the system's behavior expresses a coherent policy: a mapping from situations to verdicts that is invariant while a situation's morally relevant features are preserved, and sensitive when they change. We introduce four structural conditions for such coherent policies: verdict stability, monotonicity, decisiveness, and Pareto viability. Together they measure a form of moral competence that is evaluable from behavior alone, without reference to a moral standard or expert baseline, forming a structural floor for alignment rather than a normative target. We demonstrate the methodology on three simulated deployments featuring LLM-based agents facing moral dilemmas. Evaluating nine frontier models under a factorial design of five paraphrases, five escalation levels, and three dominance conditions, we show no model expresses a coherent policy across the three deployments: surface-form perturbation alone produces verdict-rate shifts of up to $99$ percentage points at a single escalation level, and a model's success on one scenario does not predict its competence on another. This suggests LLM-based agents are not currently the kind of object to which alignment can meaningfully apply.

</details>


### [314] [Towards Efficient Evaluation of Evolutionary Transfer Optimization: Case Studies on Task-Parameterized Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05040)
*Yanchen Li,Xiaoming Xue,Kay Chen Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究进化迁移优化（ETO）中的问题评估扩展性，通过将特定应用的串行计算重构为并行执行形式，显著降低运行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着进化迁移优化（ETO）扩展到更多相关任务，问题评估成为运行时间增长的主要来源，需要优化评估效率以实现可扩展性。

Method: 将评估扩展分为两个层级：评估任务数量和每个任务内的工作负载。在多任务优化中，使用累积矩阵表示法重构矩阵递归运动学臂评估；在顺序迁移优化中，使用混合矩阵表示法重构点式B样条轨迹评估。

Result: 两种重构方法在数值上与参考评估高度一致，分别实现了256.72倍和93.91倍的端到端加速。

Conclusion: 问题侧重构是实现可扩展进化迁移优化的实用途径，相关实现和实验脚本已开源以支持可复现性和重用。

Abstract: As evolutionary transfer optimization (ETO) scales to larger collections of related tasks, problem evaluation can become a major source of runtime growth. This work studies problem-side evaluation scaling in task-parameterized applications and reformulates application-specific serial computations into forms suitable for parallel execution. We organize evaluation scaling into two levels: the number of evaluated tasks and the workload within each task. In multi-task optimization, matrix-recursive kinematic-arm evaluation is reformulated using an accumulation-matrix representation of cumulative link directions. In sequential transfer optimization, pointwise B-spline trajectory evaluation is reformulated using a blending-matrix representation for trajectory and collision computations. Both reformulations maintain close numerical agreement with their reference evaluations and substantially reduce runtime, yielding $256.72\times$ and $93.91\times$ end-to-end speedups, respectively. These results demonstrate problem-side reformulation as a practical route toward scalable ETO. Both application implementations and experimental scripts are released as open source to support reproducibility and reuse.

</details>


### [315] [MePo++: Unifying Representation Refinement and Reconciliation for General Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05075)
*Guanglong Sun,Kanglei Zhou,Liyuan Wang,Qi Cheng,Hongwei Yan,Shuang Cui,Hang Su,Jun Zhu,Yi Zhong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MePo++ 是一个统一的预训练后框架，通过 MetaPrep（提升表示可塑性）和 StreamAlign（增强表示稳定性）两个组件，解决了预训练模型在通用持续学习（GCL）中存在的上游预训练与下游适应不匹配及输出对齐不可靠的问题，显著提升了模型在无任务标识数据流中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用持续学习（GCL）要求在无任务标识、无明确边界且无法重复访问历史数据的演化数据流中学习，极具挑战性。虽然预训练模型（PTM）提供了丰富的先验知识，但现有方法直接适配预训练表示，忽略了上游预训练与下游持续适应之间的错位，以及在模糊数据流下传统输出对齐的不可靠性。

Method: 提出 MePo++ 框架，包含两个核心组件：1) MetaPrep：通过在伪持续序列上进行无监督元细化，提高表示的可塑性以适应持续学习；2) StreamAlign：通过将演化的在线特征与稳定的预训练几何结构进行协调，增强表示的稳定性。该框架旨在适配前提升表示的可学习性，并在持续学习过程中保持对齐。

Result: 在多种预训练模型、数据集及持续学习基线上的实验表明，MePo++ 在基于预训练模型的通用持续学习任务中表现出一致的有效性和泛化能力，成功平衡了新概念学习的可塑性与数据流演化中的稳定性。

Conclusion: MePo++ 通过表示细化和协调机制，有效 bridging 了预训练知识与下游通用持续学习之间的鸿沟，使预训练模型能够在演化数据流中既保持对新概念的可塑性，又维持表示的稳定性，为 PTM 驱动的 GCL 提供了通用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: General continual learning (GCL) aims to learn from evolving data streams without task identities, explicit boundaries, or repeated access to previous data, making it a realistic yet challenging setting for continual intelligence. Although pretrained models (PTMs) provide rich prior knowledge for addressing the limited supervision and non-stationary nature of GCL, existing PTM-based methods often directly adapt pretrained representations and overlook two critical gaps: the misalignment between upstream pretraining and downstream continual adaptation, and the unreliability of conventional output alignment under blurry streams. Here we propose MePo++, a unified post-training framework that bridges pretrained knowledge and downstream GCL through representation refinement and reconciliation. MePo++ introduces two complementary components: MetaPrep, which improves representation plasticity for continual adaptation through unsupervised meta-refinement over pseudo continual sequences; and StreamAlign, which reinforces representation stability by reconciling evolving online features with a stable pretrained geometry. By improving representation learnability before adaptation and preserving alignment during continual learning, MePo++ enables PTMs to remain both plastic for new concepts and stable over evolving streams. Experiments across diverse PTMs, datasets, and continual learning baselines demonstrate the consistent effectiveness and generality of MePo++ for PTM-based GCL. Our code is available at https://github.com/SunGL001/MePo_Plus.

</details>


### [316] [TruthInsightBench: An Evidence-Grounded Benchmark for Automated Evaluation of Open-Ended Scientific Discovery Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05079)
*Zhibo Yang,Chen Zhang,Yuewei Zhang,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TruthInsightBench，一个面向科学发现而非复现的基准，用于评估自主编码AI代理在盲数据上形成可验证科学主张的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准围绕隐藏目标研究构建，奖励复现结果，但执行预设分析不等同于做出科学发现，缺乏对AI代理真实科学判断能力的评估。

Method: 构建包含40个盲任务的基准，覆盖10个科学领域，仅暴露中性科学目标和冻结数据，隐藏结论与分析路径；采用固定LLM评判器，基于6维度29项证据成熟度指标自动评分。

Result: 四个编码代理得分集中在58.4-60.3（满分100），无显著差异；具备基本分析与文档能力，但缺乏控制、稳健性、可证伪性及跨数据集泛化等关键科学判断行为。

Conclusion: 当前瓶颈在于科学判断而非编码能力，真实科学发现仍不可达；TruthInsightBench为衡量该差距提供可量化目标。

Abstract: Autonomous coding agents are increasingly proposed as AI-scientist systems that conduct analyses and write research reports, but executing a prescribed analysis is not the same as making a discovery. Existing benchmarks are configured for reproduction: tasks, data, and rubrics are built around a hidden target study, and recovery of its result is rewarded. We present TruthInsightBench, a benchmark configured for discovery. Its 40 blind tasks, drawn from 40 peer-reviewed studies across 10 scientific domains, expose only a neutral scientific objective and frozen data; source conclusions, expected values, and analysis paths are withheld, leaving the agent to determine what claim the data support. A fixed LLM-based judge scores the evidentiary maturity of an agent's own claims along six dimensions, operationalized as 29 artifact-grounded items, with automated, deterministic aggregation and no per-instance human grading, so evaluation can be repeated automatically as agents evolve. On one frozen base model, four coding agents form a narrow plateau (58.4-60.3 of 100) with no statistically reliable pairwise separation: they execute and document analyses competently, with comparatively strong evidence auditability and novelty, but largely lack the discriminating acts that establish a trustworthy claim (controls, robustness, falsifiability, and cross-dataset generalization). The bottleneck is scientific judgment rather than coding, and genuine discovery remains out of reach. TruthInsightBench makes this gap a measurable target; data and scoring code are at https://github.com/TruthInsight-stack/TruthInsightBench.

</details>


### [317] [Measuring AI Accountability Through Argumentation Analysis: Can Model Reasoning Withstand Scrutiny?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05088)
*Daan R. Henselmans,Derck W. E. Prinzhorn,Arno Libert*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于辩证协议的评估方法，用于在道德模糊情境下评估大语言模型（LLM）的推理质量，发现模型在辩护其判决时表现良好，但存在推理与事后 justification 不一致、以及 retract 机制定义不清等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的AI监督方法依赖“地面真值”（ground truth）进行验证，但在道德推理领域，何为恰当的AI行为存在争议，导致现有评估隐含地回避了现实中的模糊性。

Method: 提出一种四阶段辩证协议，基于Walton的论证图式理论和Govier的论证可靠性标准，通过评估模型在面对批判性问题时为其判决构建辩护的结构质量，来替代传统的真值验证。该协议适应不同推理框架，涵盖判决前的推理和事后的辩护。

Result: 在9个前沿模型和200个高模糊性道德选择项上的评估显示：模型在所有维度上的辩护质量均高于最低标准；失败主要集中在“理由”和“充分性”上，且与认识论上的回避（hedging）相关而非论证长度；推理过程的辩护质量优于事后辩护；约20%以上的案例中，模型使用的论证图式在推理和辩护阶段不一致。

Conclusion: 该协议能有效识别不可辩护的论证（如自相矛盾、前提错误），并揭示了在AI对齐中“撤回”（retraction）角色定义的困难，表明需要更多情境化的评估方法来解决道德模糊性问题。

Abstract: AI oversight methods rely on ground truth for validation, but what constitutes appropriate AI behavior is contested. This leaves evaluation of moral reasoning in LLMs and debate-based oversight implicitly avoiding realistic ambiguity. We investigate an alternative standard designed to function despite such ambiguity: structural quality of the defence a model can mount for its verdicts in response to critical questions, measured through a four-phase dialectical protocol grounded in Walton's theory of argumentation schemes and Govier's criteria for argument cogency. The protocol is adaptive to different frames of reasoning, extends beyond multiple-choice framing, and treats both the reasoning that precedes a verdict and its post-hoc justification. Across nine frontier models and 200 high-ambiguity MoralChoice items -- $6,778$ judge-scored cells, validated against $89.6\%$ inter-judge agreement on the binary failure judgment -- models defend their reasoning well above the rubric minimum on every dimension. Failure mass concentrates on grounds and sufficiency, and correlates with epistemic hedging rather than argument length. Reasoning is better defended than post-hoc justification, on every model and every Govier dimension. The scheme a model presents in its justification differs from the one it reasoned with on a substantial share of dilemmas ($\geq 20\%$ per model), despite value-based practical reasoning dominating both tracks. The protocol catches strictly indefensible defences (self-contradiction, false premises), and it surfaces difficulties in characterizing the role of retraction in AI alignment, suggesting a need for more situated evaluations.

</details>


### [318] [Constructing and Evaluating Clinical Reasoning Trajectories for Medical Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05090)
*Yunqi Zhu,Wensheng Zhang,Xuebing Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MedTraj框架，通过构建、评估和优化医疗AI的推理轨迹（而非仅关注最终答案），显著提升了推理的连贯性并大幅降低了幻觉率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的医疗AI评估主要关注最终答案的正确性，忽视了中间推理过程的质量。在临床环境中，基于伪造证据或不合逻辑推导出的正确答案同样具有危险性，因此需要对推理轨迹进行精细化评估。

Method: 提出MedTraj框架：1) 从医疗资源生成结构化多步推理链；2) 将轨迹解析为临床观察、证据、推理步骤和结论，并从连贯性、证据支持、幻觉、完整性和可追溯性五个维度评分；3) 通过受控错误注入建立推理失败与质量下降的因果联系；4) 利用边际贡献分析进行步骤级过滤；5) 采用质量加权上下文学习，在推理时将评估反馈给模型。

Result: 在CareQA、PubMedQA和CECMed数据集上，轨迹上下文使推理连贯性提升了0.029至0.041。在CECMed上，质量加权上下文使正确率较零样本基线翻倍，幻觉率降低了87%。边际贡献分析显示，少数关键步骤承载了大部分质量信号，且推理链超过四步后收益递减。

Conclusion: 将推理轨迹作为核心评估和优化对象，结合质量加权上下文学习，能有效提升医疗AI代理的推理质量和可靠性，证明了细粒度推理评估在临床AI应用中的关键价值。

Abstract: Evaluation of medical artificial intelligence agents remains predominantly answer-centric, assessing only the correctness of final outputs while overlooking the quality of intermediate reasoning. In clinical settings, however, a correct answer reached through fabricated evidence or incoherent logic is as dangerous as an incorrect one. We propose MedTraj, a framework that treats reasoning trajectories as critical objects for construction, evaluation, and optimization. The pipeline generates structured multi-step reasoning chains from medical reasoning sources. Each trajectory is then parsed into clinical observations, evidence, numbered reasoning steps, and a final conclusion, and scored across five quality dimensions: coherence, evidence support, hallucination, completeness, and traceability. Controlled error injection introduces targeted faults into otherwise correct trajectories to establish causal links between specific reasoning failures and measurable quality degradation. Building on this, step-level filtering based on marginal contribution identifies which individual reasoning steps drive or undermine trajectory quality. Finally, quality-weighted context learning feeds trajectory evaluations back into the model at inference time, allowing it to learn from both strong and weak reasoning demonstrations. Experiments across CareQA, PubMedQA, and CECMed demonstrate that trajectory context consistently improves reasoning coherence, with gains of +0.029 to +0.041 over a zero-shot baseline. On CECMed, quality-weighted context nearly doubles the correctness over the zero-shot baseline while cutting the hallucination ratio by 87%. Marginal-contribution analysis further shows that a small minority of reasoning steps carry most of the quality signal, and that extending chains beyond four steps yields diminishing returns.

</details>


### [319] [LLM-Guided Program Evolution for Circle Packing: Breaking 10 Packomania Records for $28](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05093)
*Wes Sander*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“发现循环”（Discovery Loop）系统，利用大语言模型（LLM）迭代进化优化算法，在Packomania圆形填充基准测试中，以极低成本刷新了10个N值（101-114）的最佳已知解，提升幅度达2.4%-5.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在通过轻量级系统实现自动化科学发现的民主化，利用LLM的推理能力自动探索和优化复杂的算法设计空间，克服传统人工设计算法的局限性。

Method: 构建一个基于LLM的迭代闭环系统：从简单种子求解器出发，LLM依据结果记分板和历史思路提出算法改进候选；通过独立验证器评估候选，保留改进方案并 discard 失败尝试；引入自适应平台检测机制以优化成本效率。

Result: 在Packomania圆形填充问题（最大化单位正方形内N个可变半径圆的半径之和）中，系统在15次迭代内（总LLM成本仅27.72美元）成功改进了N=101至114范围内10个实例的最佳已知解，性能提升2.4%-5.4%，结果已获Packomania独立收录。

Conclusion: Discovery Loop证明了LLM驱动的系统能以极低成本高效地自动发现 superior 优化算法，为自动化科学发现提供了可扩展且经济可行的新范式。

Abstract: We present Discovery Loop, a lightweight system that uses a large language model (LLM) to iteratively evolve optimization algorithms. Starting from a simple seed solver, the LLM proposes algorithmic improvements guided by a scoreboard of results and a history of prior ideas. Each candidate is evaluated against an independent verifier; improvements are kept and failures discarded. Applied to the Packomania circle-packing benchmark (csqv: maximize the sum of radii of N variable-radius circles in the unit square), the system improved the best known solutions for 10 values of N in the range 101-114, with gains of 2.4%-5.4% over prior records, all within 15 iterations and at a total LLM cost of $27.72. These results have been independently accepted by Packomania. We describe the method, analyze cost-efficiency dynamics including an adaptive plateau-detection mechanism, and discuss implications for democratizing automated scientific discovery.

</details>


### [320] [ProCA: Progressive Contrastive Alignment for Robust EEG Visual Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05094)
*Kanglei Zhou,Chunyan Lan,Dongyang Li,Jun Zhu,Liyuan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对脑电图（EEG）视觉解码中神经信号与语义表示对齐不稳定的问题，提出了一种名为ProCA的渐进式对比对齐框架，通过自适应调整语义监督和结构一致性插值，显著提升了跨主体及持续适应场景下的解码性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的EEG解码方法依赖固定的视觉或文本锚点，导致在面对跨试验、跨主体及不同学习阶段时，语义关系与变化的EEG表示发生错位；且结构无关的扰动可能扭曲关键的EEG成分，限制了模型的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出渐进式对比对齐（ProCA）框架：1) 将冻结的视觉 - 语言先验逐步细化为感知EEG特性的语义关系，实现自适应神经 - 语义对齐；2) 引入结构一致性插值，依据通道和时间重要性约束特征混合，防止语义失真。

Result: 在主体相关、主体独立、严格跨主体迁移及持续适应四种设置下，ProCA分别实现了平均相对Top-1/Top-5增益：7.4%/3.9%、10.0%/4.6%、28.1%/17.8%和16.8%/11.6%。

Conclusion: ProCA作为一种统一且模型无关的框架，有效解决了固定语义监督导致的优化偏差问题，通过渐进式对齐和结构约束显著提升了EEG视觉解码的鲁棒性和准确性。

Abstract: Electroencephalogram (EEG) visual decoding aims to recover visual semantics from non-invasive neural time-series signals, for which robust alignment between noisy neural responses and stable semantic representations is key to achieving high-performance decoding. Despite recent advances in contrastive learning, robust EEG decoding remains challenging because existing methods rely on fixed visual or textual anchors whose semantic relations may become misaligned with EEG representations that vary across trials, subjects, and learning stages. Our empirical evidence shows that this instability appears across both standard EEG decoding protocols and more challenging robustness settings, including strict cross-subject transfer and realistic personalized continual adaptation. We provide a formal analysis showing that fixed semantic supervision can bias optimization when EEG-specific relations evolve, and that structure-agnostic perturbations may distort semantically important EEG components. To address these issues, we propose Progressive Contrastive Alignment (ProCA), a unified and model-agnostic framework for adaptive neural-semantic alignment. ProCA progressively refines class-level contrastive supervision from frozen vision-language priors to EEG-aware semantic relations, and introduces structure-consistent interpolation to constrain feature mixing according to channel-wise and temporal importance. Across subject-dependent, subject-independent, strict cross-subject transfer, and continual adaptation settings, ProCA achieves average relative Top-1/Top-5 gains of 7.4%/3.9%, 10.0%/4.6%, 28.1%/17.8%, and 16.8%/11.6%, respectively.

</details>


### [321] [Compact Bellman-Grounded Cognitive Maps for Cost-Aware Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05104)
*Yuzhe Han,Mingkun Xu,Yujie Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了BCM模型，通过自监督贝尔曼目标和紧凑坐标编码，将可加性路径成本融入可复用的认知地图中，在大规模环境下实现了接近最优的路径规划性能，同时显著降低了内存占用并支持动态目标查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的类脑认知地图模型虽然能复用地图，但未显式基于可加的异质路径成本进行引导，且随着环境规模扩大，基于状态索引或高秩谱构建的方法面临严重的存储效率问题，难以兼顾生物灵活性与最优路径规划。

Method: 提出BCM（Bellman-grounded Cognitive Map）模型，利用自监督的贝尔曼接地目标函数和紧凑坐标编码，将局部边成本嵌入到可复用的认知地图表示中，支持在不进行每目标重新训练的情况下适应变化的目标查询。

Result: 在节点数达1600的加权网格上，BCM保持了100%的成功率，平均路径差距仅为5%（相比精确Dijkstra算法），而基于连通性的谱基线差距约为45%；当图规模从400节点增至3600节点时，BCM的内存占用呈次线性增长，同时保持具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 研究证明可加性路径成本可以被写入紧凑且可复用的认知地图表示中，有效弥合了生物导航的灵活性与最优路径规划之间的差距，为复杂环境下的可扩展导航提供了新范式。

Abstract: Biological agents navigate familiar environments not by re-solving routes for each new goal, but by reusing a learned map built once and read off as goals change. Existing artificial cognitive-map models mimic this reuse, yet their guidance is not explicitly grounded in additive heterogeneous route costs. Furthermore, they often struggle with memory efficiency: representative state-indexed and high-rank spectral constructions incur substantial storage growth as the environment scales. We present BCM, which grounds a reusable cognitive map in local edge costs through a self-supervised Bellman-grounded objective and a compact coordinate encoding, supporting changing goal queries without per-goal retraining. On weighted grids of up to $N=1600$ nodes, BCM maintains full success and only a 5\% mean Gap relative to exact Dijkstra search, compared with about $45\%$ for a connectivity-based spectral baseline. Notably, as the graph size increases from $N=400$ to $N=3600$, its memory footprint grows sublinearly while maintaining competitive performance, making our method scalable to complex environments. Together, these results show that additive route costs can be written into a compact, reusable cognitive-map representation, bridging the gap between biological flexibility and optimal path planning.

</details>


### [322] [Unifying ICL, SFT, KL-Regularized RL Through a Bayesian Lens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05111)
*Junxin Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出统一的贝叶斯视角，将SFT、ICL、RLHF等多种大模型训练范式归结为“构建后验分布+前向KL投影”的两步模板，解释了它们之间的内在联系与差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型的训练与评估范式（如SFT、ICL、RLHF等）常被视为本质不同，且部分实证结果（如少样本提示对RL微调模型的影响）令人困惑，缺乏统一的理论框架进行解释。

Method: 构建贝叶斯/吉布斯后验分布（基于先验模型与效用信号），并通过前向KL散度将其投影到参数化族（权重内或上下文内）；分五部分形式化推导各方法在该框架下的等价性与差异性。

Result: 证明了少样本ICL与SFT是后验预测的摊销投影；KL正则化RLHF、奖励加权SFT/ICL及优势加权SFT均为基于奖励或优势诱导后验的前向KL投影实例；明确了等价性成立的边界（目标函数与一阶更新）及不成立的边界（学习信号来源与粒度）。

Conclusion: 现代推理流水线可理解为“后验设计+投影”过程；冷启动或监督预热对重要性加权KL投影具有实践必要性；DeepSeek-R1及o1类模型结合了测试时贝叶斯搜索与训练时KL摊销。

Abstract: Large language models are now trained and evaluated under a diverse set of paradigms: supervised fine-tuning (SFT), few-shot in-context learning (ICL), KL-regularized RLHF/RLVR, on-policy distillation (OPD), and test-time reasoning with search and chain-of-thought. These methods are often discussed as fundamentally different, and recent empirical results--such as the mixed impact of few-shot prompting on RL-tuned reasoning models--can appear puzzling. This note develops a Bayesian perspective that puts these procedures on the same footing. At the core is a two-step template: (i) construct a (generalized) Bayes or Gibbs posterior q* over outputs or actions given a context, using a prior/reference model and a utility signal (log-likelihood, reward, or advantage); and (ii) approximate q* by a forward-KL projection onto a parametric family, either in-weights (SFT/RL) or in-context (ICL). Part I formalizes few-shot ICL and SFT as amortized and-weights projections onto the Bayes posterior predictive. Parts II-IV show that KL-regularized RLHF/RLVR, reward-weighted SFT, reward-weighted ICL (RW-ICL), and advantage-weighted SFT (AWSFT) are all instances of forward-KL projection onto posteriors induced by rewards or advantages. We disentangle where these equivalences hold (objectives and first-order updates) and where they do not (source and granularity of the learning signal). Part V sketches implications for modern reasoning pipelines: RLHF/RLVR recipes as "posterior design + projection", why cold-start or supervised warm-up is practically unavoidable for importance-weighted KL projections, and DeepSeek-R1 and o1-style reasoning models as combining test-time Bayesian search with training-time KL amortization.

</details>


### [323] [SciDocBench: A Workflow-Centered Benchmark and Data Pipeline for Scientific Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05141)
*Shenxi Wu,Yuhong Liu,Haosong Zhang,Tongjin Zou,Yanxun Zhang,Gaochang Chen,Dun Liang,Jiaqi Wang,Zhecan James Wang,Yuhang Zang,Dahua Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SciDocBench、SciDocIR和SciDocDataset，构建科学文档理解评估到训练框架，解决现有基准测试孤立评估多模态能力的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试通常孤立评估模型对文本、公式、图表等能力，无法判断多模态模型是否支持真实的科学阅读工作流，需要workflow-centered的基准测试。

Method: 构建SciDocBench基准测试（124个专家编写问题，7个能力组，19个子任务，5个科学领域，496个评估实例）；提出SciDocIR类型化证据图表示；构建SciDocDataset（15K监督微调样本，8K强化学习样本）。

Result: 最强评估系统仅得62.6/100分，在文档感知、证据定位、验证和跨文档推理方面存在明显弱点。

Conclusion: SciDocBench、SciDocIR和SciDocDataset共同形成评估到训练框架，用于诊断和改进科学文档助手。

Abstract: Scientific papers require models to reason jointly over text, equations, figures, tables, code, and datasets while preserving the provenance of supporting evidence. Existing benchmarks typically evaluate these capabilities in isolation, leaving unclear whether multimodal models can support realistic scientific-reading workflows. We introduce SciDocBench, a workflow-centered benchmark for scientific document understanding. It contains 124 expert-authored and difficulty-screened questions organized into seven research-assistant capability groups and 19 subtasks across five scientific domains. Each question is instantiated under four matched conditions combining English or Chinese questions with all-images-first or interleaved document representations, yielding 496 evaluation instances for controlled analysis. The strongest evaluated system achieves only 62.6/100, with pronounced weaknesses in document perception, evidence grounding, verification, and cross-document reasoning. To translate these diagnostics into scalable training signals, we introduce SciDocIR, a typed evidence-graph representation that preserves scientific document objects, layout and cross-reference relations, and provenance. Building on SciDocIR, we construct SciDocDataset, comprising approximately 15K supervised fine-tuning samples and 8K reinforcement-learning samples across 14 verifiable subtasks. Together, SciDocBench, SciDocIR, and SciDocDataset form an evaluation-to-training framework for diagnosing and improving scientific-document assistants. The project page is available at https://github.com/InternLM/SciDocBench.

</details>


### [324] [Large Language Models for HVAC Operations in Building Energy Systems: A Critical Review of Methods, Applications, and Deployment Readiness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05314)
*Alexander Neubauer,Tianzhen Hong,Han Li,Mengbo Yu,Amin Darbandi,Yannick Fürst,Martin Kriegel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统回顾了2023年至2026年3月间66篇关于大语言模型（LLM）在暖通空调（HVAC）运营中应用的研究，发现LLM目前主要适合作为语义和工作流辅助层，而非自主控制器，且尚无研究达到可立即行业部署的成熟度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑自动化系统虽产生丰富传感器数据，但因点位命名异构、元数据缺失及文档碎片化导致难以转化为实际运营洞察，亟需评估LLM在解决这些问题及优化HVAC运营中的实际潜力与成熟度。

Method: 对66篇同行评审研究进行系统综述与编码，按五个应用家族和三个LLM方法家族分类，并从证据真实性、部署就绪度及LLM与物理决策的责任边界三个维度进行评估。

Result: 研究集中在建筑能源建模（32篇），负荷预测研究不足；仅4篇达到试点级证据，无持续运营部署案例；63篇仅限研究阶段，3篇近期可用，无“即刻就绪”项目；LLM在点位名称标准化、文档辅助及工作流支持方面具近期试用价值，但在高频控制和自主代理操作方面仍不如传统ML、MPC及RL成熟。

Conclusion: 当前证据支持将LLM定位为HVAC系统的语义与工作流层而非自主控制器；未来工作应优先关注现场验证基准、运营约束下的编排评估，以及具备低延迟和可验证安全属性的LLM-MPC/RL架构。

Abstract: Building automation systems generate rich sensor data yet remain insight-poor because heterogeneous point naming, missing metadata, and fragmented documentation obstruct their operational use. This systematic review analyses and codes 66 peer-reviewed studies on large language models (LLMs) for HVAC operations published between 2023 and March 2026. Each study is classified across five application families and three LLM method families and assessed for evidence realism, deployment readiness, and the responsibility boundary between the LLM and physical HVAC decisions. The corpus is concentrated in building energy modelling (BEM, 32 of 66 papers), while load forecasting remains too sparse for subfield-level conclusions. Only four studies reach pilot-level evidence, and none reports sustained operational deployment. No study was classified as ready-now for industry adoption; three were near-term and 63 research-only. Nevertheless, several bounded, human-in-the-loop uses merit near-term trials, including point-name normalisation, document-grounded operator support, BEM workflow assistance, and advisory interfaces around physics-based controllers. Conventional machine learning (ML), model predictive control (MPC), reinforcement learning (RL) and ontology-based tools remain more adopted for high-frequency control, short-horizon numerical forecasting, and well-posed ontology mapping, while autonomous agentic operation and unvalidated occupant proxies remain research-stage. Current evidence therefore supports LLMs primarily as semantic and workflow layers rather than autonomous HVAC controllers. Future work should prioritise field-validated benchmarks, orchestration evaluation under operational constraints, and LLM-MPC/RL architectures with bounded latency and verifiable safety properties.

</details>


### [325] [A Hybrid Predictive Ensemble of Machine Learning and Deep Neural Networks for Early Cardiovascular Disease Risk Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05146)
*Balaji Venkateswaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种结合机器学习和深度神经网络集成技术的智能框架，用于心血管疾病的早期检测和预后，通过IoMT设备收集实时生理数据，并利用多种优化分类器提高诊断精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心血管疾病早期检测和预后对于改善患者预后和实现主动健康管理至关重要，传统方法在准确性、假阳性率和一致性方面存在不足，需要更高效的解决方案。

Method: 集成机器学习与深度神经网络，使用IoMT设备（ECG传感器、心率监测器、血压追踪器）收集实时数据，进行噪声降低、标准化和缺失值填补等预处理，通过特征选择识别关键健康指标，采用SVM、随机森林和XGBoost等优化分类器构建集成架构，部署在云基础设施上实现可扩展性和实时处理。

Result: 框架在预测心血管疾病风险方面表现出色，相比传统方法实现了更高的准确性、更低的假阳性率和更强的一致性，实验评估证实了其在早期风险评估和临床决策支持中的效率。

Conclusion: 结合传统机器学习和深度学习范式具有实现主动健康管理和改善患者预后的潜力，该框架为心血管疾病早期检测提供了有效的技术解决方案。

Abstract: This study introduces an intelligent framework that integrates machine learning and deep neural network ensemble techniques for early detection and prognosis of cardiovascular diseases. The system utilizes real-time physiological data collected from Internet of Medical Things (IoMT) devices, including ECG sensors, heart rate monitors, and blood pressure trackers. To ensure the accuracy and reliability of input data, preprocessing steps such as noise reduction, normalization, and missing value imputation are employed. The most significant health indicators are identified through effective feature selection methods and then processed using optimized classifiers such as Support Vector Machines (SVM), Random Forests, and eXtreme Gradient Boosting (XGBoost), which are combined in an ensemble architecture to improve diagnostic precision. The framework demonstrates remarkable performance in predicting cardiovascular disease risk, achieving higher accuracy, reduced false positives, and enhanced consistency compared to conventional methods. It is designed on a cloud-based infrastructure that ensures scalability and real-time processing for continuous patient monitoring. Experimental evaluation on real-world cardiovascular datasets confirms the framework's efficiency in early-stage risk assessment and clinical decision support. The results highlight the potential of combining traditional machine learning and deep learning paradigms to achieve proactive healthcare management and improve patient outcomes.

</details>


### [326] [Interface-Induced Trajectory Censoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03966)
*Wenbo Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 智能体评估中的工具调用率并非模型固有属性，而是模型与服务接口栈共同作用的结果；接口适配器的微小差异可导致评估结果从0到接近完美的剧烈波动，且这种“静默失败”会误导训练循环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决当前智能体评估中存在的严重测量偏差问题：观察到的工具调用率为零可能并非模型无法生成有效调用，而是服务接口在下游看到之前审查并丢弃了轨迹，导致对模型能力的误判及训练资源的浪费。

Method: 通过控制变量实验（固定权重、案例、解码及种子，仅变更服务适配器），在BFCL v4和tau-bench基准测试上量化不同聊天模板与解析器交互的影响；利用Qwen2.5-Coder系列模型进行21倍规模范围的探测，并在verl的AgentLoop中分析训练循环内的不匹配现象。

Result: 实验显示，仅更换服务适配器即可使同一模型的得分从0.00跃升至0.96；在tau-bench上，服务器解析的调用从0增至636，执行任务从0增至103。在7B模型训练循环中，45/115的生成包含完整调用但接受率为0。修复适配器虽能恢复解析机制（0->84），但对最终通过率提升不显著（53->62, p>0.05）。

Conclusion: 观测到的工具调用率是“模型-接口栈”的属性而非模型单独的属性；组件间的交互效应（而非单一组件缺陷）导致了静默失败，且这种不匹配具有规模依赖性，需在评估和训练前通过预检机制（如作者发布的98行代码）进行干预。

Abstract: Agent evaluations report a tool-call rate read off the serving stack. That number can be zero while the model is emitting well-formed calls: the interface censors the trajectory before anything downstream sees it.
  On BFCL v4's own data, executor and scorer, holding weights, cases, decoding and seeds fixed and changing only the serving adapter, the same model scores 0.00 or 0.96 / 0.19. A 2x2 over chat template and parser locates the effect exactly: both main effects are exactly zero and all of it sits in the interaction -- no component is defective, and repairing one side of the contract buys precisely nothing. On tau-bench's 115 interactive retail tasks the same swap moves server-parsed calls from 0 to 636 and tasks reaching any tool execution from 0 to 103. Our probe reproduces the funnel across a 21x scale range of Qwen2.5-Coder: the server parses 0/100 at every size while well-formed emitted calls rise to 80/100 at 32B (~72 after calibration against an adjudicated gold standard). Under a matched envelope, across a comparable scale span, the silent fraction stays at 0-2, a prediction committed to the repository before the run. Llama-3.1-8B's 23% rate of calling the task function itself as a tool falls to 0 under one strict:true flag.
  The mismatch reaches inside the training loop, and its consequence is scale-dependent: in verl's AgentLoop at 7B, 45 of 115 generations carry a complete call; 0 are accepted, 0 execute, 0 return an observation. At 1.5B the same zero is over-determined, so we report the two scales separately. At evaluation time, repairing the adapter restores the mechanism but not a significant outcome gain: parsing 0->84, rescues 0->9, pass rate 53->62 (n.s.). We release a 98-line preflight check that catches every silent failure here. The observed tool-call rate is not a property of the model alone; it is a property of the model-interface stack that measures it.

</details>


### [327] [Technical Manual for a Toolkit for Measuring Contextual Individuation in Transformer Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05333)
*José Luciano Verçosa Marques,Frederico Jorge Heitmann,Daniel Omar Perez,Marcelo Vinicius de Paula,Tárcio André dos Santos Barros*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个名为“桥接形式”（bridge form）的开源工具包，旨在通过控制变量（固定词形、变化语境和词义）来严格测试 Transformer 语言模型是否能在深层中区分同一单词的不同语境含义，本文仅作为方法论和实施参考，未报告具体实证结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管 Transformer 语言模型在嵌入层为词型分配单一且与语境无关的向量，但学界普遍认为其在后续层中能根据语境区分单词的具体出现实例。然而，缺乏一种能在固定词形的同时，以受控且带标签的方式改变语境和预期词义的构造来清晰地验证这一信念。

Method: 构建围绕“桥接形式”（即在两个或多个主题领域中重复出现、形式不变但词义不同的单个书面词）的开源工具包。流程包括：桥接形式及其源领域的声明式规范、从 Wikipedia 获取语料、定位出现实例、提取逐层表示、计算模型表示空间中域对间的轮廓系数（silhouette measurement）以衡量分离度，以及配对可视化协议。每个设计选择均针对特定的方法论失败模式（如sense contamination、多组偏差、子词分词错位、降维图中的轴可比性伪影等）进行了论证。

Result: 本文未报告或解释在任何特定模型或桥接形式集上运行该工具包的实证结果。其产出为一个完整的方法论文档、实施工具包、源代码及用于测试的语料库，这些资源已单独归档并拥有持久标识符。

Conclusion: 该手稿定位为方法论和实施参考，旨在为后续利用该工具包生成和解释实证结果的研究提供可引用的仪器（instrument），从而推动对语言模型语境化表示能力的严谨评估。

Abstract: A transformer language model assigns a single, context-independent vector to a word type at its embedding layer, yet is widely believed to individuate that word's occurrences by context in its later layers. Testing this belief cleanly requires a construct that holds the word form fixed while its context and intended sense vary in a controlled, labeled way. This manual documents an open toolkit built around such a construct, which we call a bridge form: a single written word that recurs, unchanged, across two or more subject domains with a different sense in each. We describe, and justify, every stage of the pipeline: the declarative specification of bridge forms and their source domains, corpus acquisition from Wikipedia, occurrence localization, layer-wise representation extraction, a domain-pairwise silhouette measurement of separation in the model's representation space, and a paired visualization protocol. Each design choice is presented together with the methodological failure mode it is meant to avoid (sense contamination from overly broad category labels, the multi-group bias of the silhouette coefficient, subword-tokenization misalignment, and axis-comparability artifacts in dimensionality-reduced plots, among others). This manuscript is a methodological and implementation reference: it does not report or interpret empirical outcomes of running the toolkit on any particular model or bridge-form set. The toolkit, its full source, and the corpora used to exercise it are archived separately (Section 9) under a persistent identifier, and are intended to be cited as an instrument by studies that use it to produce and interpret empirical results.

</details>


### [328] [The Mirror Agent Model: a Bayesian Architecture for Interpretable Agent Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05190)
*Michele Persiani,Thomas Hellström*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"Mirror Agent Model"的新架构，旨在生成可解释的行为和解释，通过将观察者模型定义为代理模型的镜像来实现显式和隐式通信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提供对代理意图的信息性通信和产生可理解行为的一般理解，需要一种能够生成可解释行为和解释的新架构。

Method: 首先展示与代理意图的信息性通信和可理解行为生产相关的先前结果，然后通过现成的显著性方法为架构提供新的解释能力，并进行初步的定性结果分析。

Result: 通过Mirror Agent Model架构，实现了代理行为的可解释性和解释生成能力，并展示了初步的定性结果。

Conclusion: Mirror Agent Model架构为代理的可解释行为和解释生成提供了一种有效的方法，有助于理解代理的意图和行为。

Abstract: In this paper we illustrate a novel architecture generating interpretable behavior and explanations. We refer to this architecture as the Mirror Agent Model because it defines the observer model, that is the target of explicit and implicit communications, as a mirror of the agent's. With the goal of providing a general understanding of this work, we firstly show prior relevant results addressing the informative communication of agents intentions and the production of legible behavior. In the second part of the paper we furnish the architecture with novel capabilities for explanations through off-the-shelf saliency methods, followed by preliminary qualitative results.

</details>


### [329] [Does Your Agent's Memory Survive a Model Upgrade? A Controlled Study of Memory Portability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05339)
*Ankit Goyal,Jaideep Ray*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文对比了四种记忆存储格式（原始文本、RAG分块、模型压缩笔记、固定图谱）在模型升级时的迁移表现，发现固定图谱最稳定，而压缩笔记和混合嵌入的RAG系统因模型耦合或检索失败导致性能显著下降，强调了保留原始历史数据对记忆修复的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决代理（Agent）在模型升级过程中面临的“记忆迁移”难题：新模型可能误读旧笔记、混合嵌入版本破坏检索、且缺乏原始证据时修复失败，旨在量化不同记忆表示法在模型迭代中的鲁棒性。

Method: 构建48个带有随机答案代码的合成历史数据集，使用两个参数量小于100亿的开源模型，对比四种记忆策略：长上下文原始读取（LC-RAW）、检索增强生成（RAG）、模型压缩的自然语言笔记（NOTES）以及固定模式知识图谱（KG-fixed），通过精确评分评估模型替换后的准确率变化及修复能力。

Result: 固定模式知识图谱（KG-fixed）迁移最可靠，准确率波动极小（+0.0004±0.0020）；压缩笔记（NOTES）表现出强模型耦合，准确率随迁移方向不对称波动（+9.91%或-13.28%）；RAG系统中混合索引仅能挽回4.96%的性能，远低于全量重嵌入的11.90%增益；诊断显示NOTES的缺陷80%源于构建时的信息丢失，RAG的缺陷81%源于检索失败；仅靠存储修复NOTES无法在48个案例中达到90%的恢复目标，而保留原始历史可在34个案例中成功修复。

Conclusion: 模型升级时的记忆迁移具有方向特异性，固定结构化数据最稳健；为确保持续性能，必须实施严格的嵌入空间隔离测试，并强制保留原始源历史数据以支持有效的记忆修复。

Abstract: Model upgrades are routine; memory migrations are not. An agent can keep the same memory store and still forget: a new model may interpret old notes differently, mixed embedding versions may break retrieval, and repair may fail without the original evidence. We compare memory as the same history is preserved verbatim for long-context reading (LC-RAW), divided into chunks for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), compressed by a model into natural-language notes (NOTES), or normalized into a fixed-schema knowledge graph (KG-fixed). The study uses 48 synthetic histories with randomized answer codes, exact scoring, and two open-weight models with sub 10 billion parameters.
  Our measurements show that fixed-schema structures transfer reliably, with KG-fixed accuracy changing by only $+0.0004 \pm 0.0020$ following a writer swap. Conversely, compressed NOTES exhibit high model coupling, with accuracy shifting asymmetrically by $+9.91$ or $-13.28$ percentage points depending on the specific migration direction. In RAG systems, partial embedding migrations using a 50/50 mixed index capture only a 4.96-point accuracy improvement, forfeiting the majority of the 11.90-point gain achieved through full re-embedding. Diagnostic decomposition attributes 80% ($0.467 \pm 0.014$) of the NOTES accuracy deficit to information lost during initial construction, whereas retrieval failures drive 81% ($0.364 \pm 0.012$) of the RAG deficit. Finally, store-only repair of NOTES fails to reach a 90% performance recovery target in all 48 test cases, whereas retaining the raw source history enables successful recovery in 34 of 48 cases for one tested direction. These findings highlight the necessity of direction-specific migration testing, strict embedding space isolation, and the retention of source histories for memory repair.

</details>


### [330] [What Matters in On-Policy Distillation? A Perspective on Data Efficiency and Data Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05198)
*Zhinan Hou,Jiaqi Zhang,Xunliang Cai,Keyou You*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过实证研究发现，在策略蒸馏（OPD）中，仅使用少量高难度样本（甚至1个样本）进行训练，其效果可媲美大规模数据集，关键在于长思维链（CoT）路径而非高令牌熵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 策略蒸馏（OPD）虽已成为提升大语言模型推理能力的流行范式，但其数据-centric机制（特别是数据效率和数据选择）尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 首先探究极端设置下的“1-shot OPD”效果；其次分析驱动学生模型提升的核心因素（对比令牌熵与CoT长度）；最后基于发现提出一种仅选择高难度样本（包括超出教师模型能力的样本）的简单数据选择方法，并在4个不同规模的模型上进行实验验证。

Result: 1. 1-shot OPD在所有采样样本中均有效，且高难度样本往往带来更显著的性能提升。2. 模型提升并非由高令牌熵驱动，而是由难题自然生成的更长CoT路径驱动，这有助于保持与教师模型的长期对齐并学习反思等关键思维模式。3. 仅使用8个精选的高难度样本训练学生模型，其性能即可匹配17K样本数据集的基线效果。

Conclusion: 在OPD中，数据质量（特别是样本难度和CoT长度）远比数据数量重要；通过筛选高难度样本构建的小规模数据集即可实现高效的知识蒸馏。

Abstract: On-Policy Distillation (OPD) has emerged as a widely adopted post-training paradigm for enhancing large language models in reasoning domains. However, the data-centric mechanisms in OPD remain relatively underexplored. This paper presents a empirical study of data efficiency and data selection in OPD. We begin by investigating an extreme setting: training OPD on only one example, namely 1-shot OPD. Surprisingly, we find that 1-shot OPD is consistently effective across all sampled training examples and harder examples often yield superior performance gain. We next investigate what actually drives the student model's improvement in the training data. Our analysis reveals that the improvement is not driven by high token entropy, but the longer CoT paths which hard problems naturally generate. Training on longer CoT can help maintain closer alignment with the teacher over a long reasoning horizon, and learn critical thinking patterns usually missing in short CoTs, such as reflection (e.g., ``Alternatively''). Based on these insights, we propose a simple data selection method that selects only hard examples for training, where even ``unsolvable'' examples that completely exceed the teacher's capability can be successfully used. Our experiments conducted on four models ranging from 1.5B to 7B show that training the student model on only 8 selected hard examples matches the performance of the 17K dataset baseline.

</details>


### [331] [Multi-Step Tool-Calling over Korean Open Public APIs: A Benchmark and a Data-Synthesis Recipe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05395)
*Dain Kim,Eungi Cho,Kyumin Kim,Shinyeong Noh,Kyuseong Lim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对开源模型在政府API多步调用任务中表现不佳且缺乏基准的问题，提出了韩国开放公共API基准（KOPA-Bench）和基于实时执行验证的动态图数据合成方法（EDGE），通过该方法训练出的9B模型性能显著提升，接近同系列未微调的27B模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据主权法规要求公共机构部署本地开源LLM代理以调用政府API，但现有开源模型在多步工具调用场景中表现欠佳，且缺乏专门的基准来衡量这一差距。

Method: 提出KOPA-Bench基准（含145个真实任务）和EDGE方法：构建工具输出与输入关系的动态图，仅保留经实时API调用验证成功的链接，并遍历这些链接合成可执行的多步轨迹数据；随后利用GRPO算法在该数据集上对9B模型进行微调。

Result: 经EDGE数据微调的9B模型在KOPA-Bench和BFCL基准上表现大幅提升，性能几乎媲美同系列未微调的27B模型。

Conclusion: EDGE方法通过基于实时执行验证的动态图合成高质量多步工具调用数据，有效缩小了开源小模型与大型模型在复杂API调用任务中的性能差距，为数据主权场景下的本地部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Data-sovereignty regulations increasingly require public institutions to deploy open-source, on-premise LLM agents that chain multiple tool-calls across live government APIs. However, open-source models consistently underperform in this multi-step setting, and no existing benchmark measures the gap. We introduce the Korean Open Public API Benchmark (KOPA-Bench), comprising 145 real-world tasks. To close this gap, we present EDGE, an Execution-grounded Dynamic Graph for tool-calling data synthEsis driven by live execution. EDGE builds a graph of how each tool's output can feed another's input, keeps only the links that succeed when actually called against the live APIs, and traverses these verified links to synthesize executable multi-step trajectories. Fine-tuned via GRPO on the resulting dataset, our 9B model nearly matches the untuned 27B model from the same family, improving substantially not only on KOPA-Bench but also on the BFCL benchmark.

</details>


### [332] [CABAL: Multi-Agent Simulacra for Tracing the Effects of Collusive Bidding in Peer Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05227)
*Jicheng Zhou,Kemou Li,Kahim Wong,Zheyuan Li,Zhuan Shi,Fengpeng Li,Haiwei Wu,Jiantao Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种端到端的多智能体模拟框架（\alg），通过配置具有诚实或共谋策略的LLM驱动审稿人代理，在固定会议环境下研究审稿分配完整性。研究发现，基于亲和力的共谋投标策略能使目标论文被捕获率翻倍，且共谋审稿人评分显著高于诚实审稿人，但现有检测器对此类共谋行为的识别能力有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将投标、审稿人分配和评审操纵视为独立阶段，忽略了共谋投标的生命周期效应；同时，现实世界中共谋意图不可观察且缺乏同一会议的对照实验，导致难以评估共谋投标对审稿分配完整性的实际影响。

Method: 引入\alg框架，构建端到端多智能体模拟环境，固定会议配置，利用LLM驱动审稿人代理执行诚实或共谋策略；设计基于亲和力的共谋投标策略，利用审稿人 - 论文相互亲和力构建共谋环并选择目标论文，实现与专业知识一致的定向攻击。

Result: 控制实验显示，共谋投标使目标论文捕获率增加一倍以上，被分配的共谋审稿人对目标论文的评分比诚实共同审稿人高出约2分；会议整体影响相对较小；现有投标阶段检测器证据有限，原生正投标图受良性亲和力干扰，仅"Very-High-only"诊断视图能实现精确但低覆盖率的局部恢复。

Conclusion: 共谋投标对目标论文具有显著操纵效果，但现有检测机制难以有效识别；基于亲和力的共谋策略更具隐蔽性，需开发更精准的检测方法来保障审稿分配完整性。

Abstract: Recent reports during the AAAI-27 review cycle highlight the risk of reviewers coordinating bids for reciprocal assignment advantage. Prior work treats bidding, reviewer assignment, and review manipulation as separate stages, leaving the lifecycle effects of collusive bidding unclear. Real-world analysis is further constrained by typically unobservable collusive intent and the lack of counterfactuals for the same conference. Motivated by this gap, we introduce \alg, an end-to-end multi-agent simulacra framework for studying reviewer assignment integrity by holding the conference environment fixed and configuring LLM-driven reviewer agents with honest or collusive policies. We further develop an affinity-guided collusive bidding strategy that uses mutual reviewer-paper affinities to construct collusion rings and select target papers, producing expertise-consistent rather than arbitrarily targeted attacks. Controlled experiments show that collusive bidding more than doubles target-paper capture and that assigned colluders score target papers about two points higher than honest co-reviewers, while conference-wide effects remain comparatively modest. Evaluated bid-phase detectors provide only limited evidence of collusion: in a fixed-triplet detector stress test, native positive-bid graphs are confounded by benign affinity, while a Very-High-only diagnostic view enables precise but low-coverage local recovery.

</details>


### [333] [ACE: Adaptive Calibration-Free Expert Skipping for MoE-based LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05228)
*Zukang Xu,Zhixiong Zhao,Xing Hu,Jiangyong Yu,Houji Wen,Jun Li,Zhe Jiang,Dawei Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了ACE框架，这是一种无需训练、无需校准且保留检查点的混合专家（MoE）模型推理优化方法。通过结合全局谱代理（GSP）和路由器条件细化（RCR）两种机制，ACE能在推理时自适应地跳过低贡献的专家槽位，在大幅减少计算冗余的同时，显著优于现有的静态和动态基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的混合专家（MoE）架构采用固定的top-k路由策略，导致每个token激活相同数量的专家槽位，产生大量冗余计算。而现有的专家跳过方法通常依赖路由器置信度、校准数据或额外的训练，无法可靠地估计被路由专家的实际贡献。

Method: 提出ACE框架，包含两个互补组件：1) 全局谱代理（GSP），从耦合的门控、上投影和下投影以及RMSNorm缩放中估计全局变换能力；2) 路由器条件细化（RCR），基于中心化的路由器权重构建专家特定的方向原型，并评估专家在路由偏好方向上的响应。推理时，仅当两种视图均判定专家为低贡献时才跳过（始终保留top-1专家），所有统计量离线计算，在线仅进行查表和轻量标量运算。
